[A] OsdUnivar4x4(in float u, out float B[4], out float D[4])
[A] float t = u;
[A] float s = 1.0f - u;
[A] float A0 = s * s;
[A] float A1 = 2 * s * t;
[A] float A2 = t * t;
[A] B[0] = s * A0;
[A] B[1] = t * A0 + s * A1;
[A] B[2] = t * A1 + s * A2;
[A] B[3] = t * A2;
[A] D[0] =    - A0;
[A] D[1] = A0 - A1;
[A] D[2] = A1 - A2;
[A] D[3] = A2;
[A] OsdUnivar4x4(in float u, out float B[4], out float D[4], out float C[4])
[A] A0 =   - s;
[A] A1 = s - t;
[A] A2 = t;
[A] C[0] =    - A0;
[A] C[1] = A0 - A1;
[A] C[2] = A1 - A2;
[A] C[3] = A2;

[A] // Boundary Interpolation
[A] OsdComputeBSplineBoundaryPoints(inout vec3 cpt[16], ivec3 patchParam)

[A] cpt[1] = 2*cpt[5] - cpt[9];
[A] cpt[2] = 2*cpt[6] - cpt[10];
[A] if ((boundaryMask & 2) != 0) {

[A] cpt[11] = 2*cpt[10] - cpt[9];

[A] cpt[0] = 2*cpt[1] - cpt[2];
[A] cpt[12] = 2*cpt[13] - cpt[14];
[A] OsdComputeBoxSplineTriangleBoundaryPoints(inout vec3 cpt[12], ivec3 patchParam)
[A] if (boundaryMask == 0) return;
[A] int upperBits = (boundaryMask >> 3) & 0x3;
[A] int lowerBits = boundaryMask & 7;
[A] int eBits = lowerBits;
[A] int vBits = 0;
[A] if (upperBits == 1) {
[A] vBits = eBits;
[A] eBits = 0;
[A] } else if (upperBits == 2) {
[A] //  Opposite vertex bit is edge bit rotated one to the right:
[A] vBits = ((eBits & 1) << 2) | (eBits >> 1);

[A] bool edge1IsBoundary = (eBits & 2) != 0;

[A] // BSpline
[A] // compute single-crease patch matrix
[A] OsdComputeMs(float sharpness)
[A] float s = pow(2.0f, sharpness);
[A] float s2 = s*s;
[A] float s3 = s2*s;
[A] mat4 m = mat4(
[A] 0, s + 1 + 3*s2 - s3, 7*s - 2 - 6*s2 + 2*s3, (1-s)*(s-1)*(s-1),
[A] 0,       (1+s)*(1+s),        6*s - 2 - 2*s2,       (s-1)*(s-1),
[A] 0,               1+s,               6*s - 2,               1-s,
[A] 0,                 1,               6*s - 2,                 1);
[A] m /= (s*6.0);
[A] m[0][0] = 1.0/6.0;
[A] return m;
[A] // flip matrix orientation
[A] OsdFlipMatrix(mat4 m)
[A] return mat4(m[3][3], m[3][2], m[3][1], m[3][0],
[A] m[2][3], m[2][2], m[2][1], m[2][0],
[A] m[1][3], m[1][2], m[1][1], m[1][0],
[A] m[0][3], m[0][2], m[0][1], m[0][0]);
[A] // Regular BSpline to Bezier
[A] uniform mat4 Q = mat4(
[A] 1.f/6.f, 4.f/6.f, 1.f/6.f, 0.f,
[A] 0.f,     4.f/6.f, 2.f/6.f, 0.f,
[A] 0.f,     2.f/6.f, 4.f/6.f, 0.f,
[A] 0.f,     1.f/6.f, 4.f/6.f, 1.f/6.f
[A] // Infinitely Sharp (boundary)
[A] uniform mat4 Mi = mat4(
[A] 0.f,     0.f,     1.f,     0.f
[A] // convert BSpline cv to Bezier cv
[A] OsdComputePerPatchVertexBSpline(ivec3 patchParam, int ID, vec3 cv[16],
[A] out OsdPerPatchVertexBezier result)
[A] result.patchParam = patchParam;
[A] int i = ID%4;
[A] int j = ID/4;
[A] vec3 P  = vec3(0); // 0 to 1-2^(-Sf)
[A] vec3 P1 = vec3(0); // 1-2^(-Sf) to 1-2^(-Sc)
[A] vec3 P2 = vec3(0); // 1-2^(-Sc) to 1
[A] float sharpness = OsdGetPatchSharpness(patchParam);
[A] if (sharpness > 0) {
[A] float Sf = floor(sharpness);
[A] float Sc = ceil(sharpness);
[A] float Sr = fract(sharpness);
[A] mat4 Mf = OsdComputeMs(Sf);
[A] mat4 Mc = OsdComputeMs(Sc);
[A] mat4 Mj = (1-Sr) * Mf + Sr * Mi;
[A] mat4 Ms = (1-Sr) * Mf + Sr * Mc;
[A] float s0 = 1 - pow(2, -floor(sharpness));
[A] float s1 = 1 - pow(2, -ceil(sharpness));
[A] result.vSegments = vec2(s0, s1);
[A] mat4 MUi = Q, MUj = Q, MUs = Q;
[A] mat4 MVi = Q, MVj = Q, MVs = Q;
[A] MVi = OsdFlipMatrix(Mi);
[A] MVj = OsdFlipMatrix(Mj);
[A] MVs = OsdFlipMatrix(Ms);
[A] MUi = Mi;
[A] MUj = Mj;
[A] MUs = Ms;
[A] MVi = Mi;
[A] MVj = Mj;
[A] MVs = Ms;
[A] MUi = OsdFlipMatrix(Mi);
[A] MUj = OsdFlipMatrix(Mj);
[A] MUs = OsdFlipMatrix(Ms);
[A] vec3 Hi[4], Hj[4], Hs[4];
[A] for (int l=0; l<4; ++l) {
[A] Hi[l] = Hj[l] = Hs[l] = vec3(0);
[A] Hi[l] += MUi[i][k] * cv[l*4 + k];
[A] Hj[l] += MUj[i][k] * cv[l*4 + k];
[A] Hs[l] += MUs[i][k] * cv[l*4 + k];
[A] P  += MVi[j][k]*Hi[k];
[A] P1 += MVj[j][k]*Hj[k];
[A] P2 += MVs[j][k]*Hs[k];
[A] result.P  = P;
[A] result.P1 = P1;
[A] result.P2 = P2;
[A] result.vSegments = vec2(0);
[A] OsdComputeBSplineBoundaryPoints(cv, patchParam);
[A] vec3 Hi[4];
[A] Hi[l] = vec3(0);
[A] Hi[l] += Q[i][k] * cv[l*4 + k];
[A] P += Q[j][k]*Hi[k];
[A] result.P1 = P;
[A] result.P2 = P;
[A] vec3 H[4];
[A] H[l] = vec3(0);
[A] H[l] += Q[i][k] * cv[l*4 + k];
[A] result.P = vec3(0);
[A] result.P += Q[j][k]*H[k];
[A] OsdEvalPatchBezier(ivec3 patchParam, vec2 UV,
[A] OsdPerPatchVertexBezier cv[16],
[A] out vec3 P, out vec3 dPu, out vec3 dPv,
[A] out vec3 N, out vec3 dNu, out vec3 dNv)
[A] //  Use the recursive nature of the basis functions to compute a 2x2 set
[A] //  of intermediate points (via repeated linear interpolation).  These
[A] //  points define a bilinear surface tangent to the desired surface at P
[A] //  and so containing dPu and dPv.  The cost of computing P, dPu and dPv
[A] //  this way is comparable to that of typical tensor product evaluation
[A] //  (if not faster).
[A] //  If N = dPu X dPv degenerates, it often results from an edge of the
[A] //  2x2 bilinear hull collapsing or two adjacent edges colinear. In both
[A] //  cases, the expected non-planar quad degenerates into a triangle, and
[A] //  the tangent plane of that triangle provides the desired normal N.
[A] //  Reduce 4x4 points to 2x4 -- two levels of linear interpolation in U
[A] //  and so 3 original rows contributing to each of the 2 resulting rows:
[A] float u    = UV.x;
[A] float uinv = 1.0f - u;
[A] float u0 = uinv * uinv;
[A] float u1 = u * uinv * 2.0f;
[A] float u2 = u * u;
[A] vec3 LROW[4], RROW[4];
[A] #ifndef OSD_PATCH_ENABLE_SINGLE_CREASE
[A] LROW[0] = u0 * cv[ 0].P + u1 * cv[ 1].P + u2 * cv[ 2].P;
[A] LROW[1] = u0 * cv[ 4].P + u1 * cv[ 5].P + u2 * cv[ 6].P;
[A] LROW[2] = u0 * cv[ 8].P + u1 * cv[ 9].P + u2 * cv[10].P;
[A] LROW[3] = u0 * cv[12].P + u1 * cv[13].P + u2 * cv[14].P;
[A] RROW[0] = u0 * cv[ 1].P + u1 * cv[ 2].P + u2 * cv[ 3].P;
[A] RROW[1] = u0 * cv[ 5].P + u1 * cv[ 6].P + u2 * cv[ 7].P;
[A] RROW[2] = u0 * cv[ 9].P + u1 * cv[10].P + u2 * cv[11].P;
[A] RROW[3] = u0 * cv[13].P + u1 * cv[14].P + u2 * cv[15].P;
[A] vec2 vSegments = cv[0].vSegments;
[A] float s = OsdGetPatchSingleCreaseSegmentParameter(patchParam, UV);
[A] for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
[A] int j = i*4;
[A] LROW[i] = u0 * cv[ j ].P + u1 * cv[j+1].P + u2 * cv[j+2].P;
[A] RROW[i] = u0 * cv[j+1].P + u1 * cv[j+2].P + u2 * cv[j+3].P;
[A] LROW[i] = u0 * cv[ j ].P1 + u1 * cv[j+1].P1 + u2 * cv[j+2].P1;
[A] RROW[i] = u0 * cv[j+1].P1 + u1 * cv[j+2].P1 + u2 * cv[j+3].P1;
[A] LROW[i] = u0 * cv[ j ].P2 + u1 * cv[j+1].P2 + u2 * cv[j+2].P2;
[A] RROW[i] = u0 * cv[j+1].P2 + u1 * cv[j+2].P2 + u2 * cv[j+3].P2;
[A] //  Reduce 2x4 points to 2x2 -- two levels of linear interpolation in V
[A] //  and so 3 original pairs contributing to each of the 2 resulting:
[A] float v    = UV.y;
[A] float vinv = 1.0f - v;
[A] float v0 = vinv * vinv;
[A] float v1 = v * vinv * 2.0f;
[A] float v2 = v * v;
[A] vec3 LPAIR[2], RPAIR[2];
[A] LPAIR[0] = v0 * LROW[0] + v1 * LROW[1] + v2 * LROW[2];
[A] RPAIR[0] = v0 * RROW[0] + v1 * RROW[1] + v2 * RROW[2];
[A] LPAIR[1] = v0 * LROW[1] + v1 * LROW[2] + v2 * LROW[3];
[A] RPAIR[1] = v0 * RROW[1] + v1 * RROW[2] + v2 * RROW[3];
[A] //  Interpolate points on the edges of the 2x2 bilinear hull from which
[A] //  both position and partials are trivially determined:
[A] vec3 DU0 = vinv * LPAIR[0] + v * LPAIR[1];
[A] vec3 DU1 = vinv * RPAIR[0] + v * RPAIR[1];
[A] vec3 DV0 = uinv * LPAIR[0] + u * RPAIR[0];
[A] vec3 DV1 = uinv * LPAIR[1] + u * RPAIR[1];
[A] int level = OsdGetPatchFaceLevel(patchParam);
[A] dPu = (DU1 - DU0) * 3 * level;
[A] dPv = (DV1 - DV0) * 3 * level;
[A] P = u * DU1 + uinv * DU0;
[A] //  Compute the normal and test for degeneracy:
[A] //  We need a geometric measure of the size of the patch for a suitable
[A] //  tolerance.  Magnitudes of the partials are generally proportional to
[A] //  that size -- the sum of the partials is readily available, cheap to
[A] //  compute, and has proved effective in most cases (though not perfect).
[A] //  The size of the bounding box of the patch, or some approximation to
[A] //  it, would be better but more costly to compute.
[A] float proportionalNormalTolerance = 0.00001f;
[A] float nEpsilon = (length(dPu) + length(dPv)) * proportionalNormalTolerance;
[A] N = cross(dPu, dPv);
[A] float nLength = length(N);
[A] if (nLength > nEpsilon) {
[A] N = N / nLength;
[A] vec3 diagCross = cross(RPAIR[1] - LPAIR[0], LPAIR[1] - RPAIR[0]);
[A] float diagCrossLength = length(diagCross);
[A] if (diagCrossLength > nEpsilon) {
[A] N = diagCross / diagCrossLength;
[A] #ifndef OSD_COMPUTE_NORMAL_DERIVATIVES
[A] dNu = vec3(0);
[A] dNv = vec3(0);
[A] //  Compute 2nd order partials of P(u,v) in order to compute 1st order partials
[A] //  for the un-normalized n(u,v) = dPu X dPv, then project into the tangent
[A] //  plane of normalized N.  With resulting dNu and dNv we can make another
[A] //  attempt to resolve a still-degenerate normal.
[A] //  We don't use the Weingarten equations here as they require N != 0 and also
[A] //  are a little less numerically stable/accurate in single precision.
[A] float B0u[4], B1u[4], B2u[4];
[A] float B0v[4], B1v[4], B2v[4];
[A] OsdUnivar4x4(UV.x, B0u, B1u, B2u);
[A] OsdUnivar4x4(UV.y, B0v, B1v, B2v);
[A] vec3 dUU = vec3(0);
[A] vec3 dVV = vec3(0);
[A] vec3 dUV = vec3(0);
[A] #ifdef OSD_PATCH_ENABLE_SINGLE_CREASE
[A] int k = 4*i + j;
[A] vec3 CV = (s <= vSegments.x) ? cv[k].P
[A] :   ((s <= vSegments.y) ? cv[k].P1
[A] : cv[k].P2);
[A] vec3 CV = cv[4*i + j].P;
[A] dUU += (B0v[i] * B2u[j]) * CV;
[A] dVV += (B2v[i] * B0u[j]) * CV;
[A] dUV += (B1v[i] * B1u[j]) * CV;
[A] dUU *= 6 * level;
[A] dVV *= 6 * level;
[A] dUV *= 9 * level;
[A] dNu = cross(dUU, dPv) + cross(dPu, dUV);
[A] dNv = cross(dUV, dPv) + cross(dPu, dVV);
[A] float nLengthInv = 1.0;
[A] nLengthInv = 1.0 / nLength;
[A] //  N may have been resolved above if degenerate, but if N was resolved
[A] //  we don't have an accurate length for its un-normalized value, and that
[A] //  length is needed to project the un-normalized dNu and dNv into the
[A] //  tangent plane of N.
[A] //  So compute N more accurately with available second derivatives, i.e.
[A] //  with a 1st order Taylor approximation to un-normalized N(u,v).
[A] float DU = (UV.x == 1.0f) ? -1.0f : 1.0f;
[A] float DV = (UV.y == 1.0f) ? -1.0f : 1.0f;
[A] N = DU * dNu + DV * dNv;
[A] nLength = length(N);
[A] nLengthInv = 1.0f / nLength;
[A] N = N * nLengthInv;
[A] //  Project derivatives of non-unit normals into tangent plane of N:
[A] dNu = (dNu - dot(dNu,N) * N) * nLengthInv;
[A] dNv = (dNv - dot(dNv,N) * N) * nLengthInv;
[A] // Gregory Basis
[A] struct OsdPerPatchVertexGregoryBasis {
[A] OsdComputePerPatchVertexGregoryBasis(ivec3 patchParam, int ID, vec3 cv,
[A] out OsdPerPatchVertexGregoryBasis result)
[A] result.P = cv;
[A] OsdEvalPatchGregory(ivec3 patchParam, vec2 UV, vec3 cv[20],
[A] float u = UV.x, v = UV.y;
[A] float U = 1-u, V = 1-v;
[A] //(0,1)                              (1,1)
[A] //   P3         e3-      e2+         P2
[A] //      15------17-------11-------10
[A] //      |        |        |        |
[A] //      |        | f3-    | f2+    |
[A] //      |       19       13        |
[A] //  e3+ 16-----18          14-----12 e2-
[A] //      |     f3+          f2-     |
[A] //      |                          |
[A] //      |     f0-         f1+      |
[A] //  e0- 2------4            8------6 e1+
[A] //      |        3 f0+    9        |
[A] //      |        |        | f1-    |
[A] //      0--------1--------7--------5
[A] //    P0        e0+      e1-         P1
[A] //(0,0)                               (1,0)
[A] float d11 = u+v;
[A] float d12 = U+v;
[A] float d21 = u+V;
[A] float d22 = U+V;
[A] OsdPerPatchVertexBezier bezcv[16];
[A] bezcv[ 5].P = (d11 == 0.0) ? cv[3]  : (u*cv[3] + v*cv[4])/d11;
[A] bezcv[ 6].P = (d12 == 0.0) ? cv[8]  : (U*cv[9] + v*cv[8])/d12;
[A] bezcv[ 9].P = (d21 == 0.0) ? cv[18] : (u*cv[19] + V*cv[18])/d21;
[A] bezcv[10].P = (d22 == 0.0) ? cv[13] : (U*cv[13] + V*cv[14])/d22;
[A] bezcv[ 0].P = cv[0];
[A] bezcv[ 1].P = cv[1];
[A] bezcv[ 2].P = cv[7];
[A] bezcv[ 3].P = cv[5];
[A] bezcv[ 4].P = cv[2];
[A] bezcv[ 7].P = cv[6];
[A] bezcv[ 8].P = cv[16];
[A] bezcv[11].P = cv[12];
[A] bezcv[12].P = cv[15];
[A] bezcv[13].P = cv[17];
[A] bezcv[14].P = cv[11];
[A] bezcv[15].P = cv[10];
[A] OsdEvalPatchBezier(patchParam, UV, bezcv, P, dPu, dPv, N, dNu, dNv);
[A] //  Convert the 12 points of a regular patch resulting from Loop subdivision
[A] //  into a more accessible Bezier patch for both tessellation assessment and
[A] //  evaluation.
[A] //  Regular patch for Loop subdivision -- quartic triangular Box spline:
[A] //                           10 --- 11
[A] //                           . .   . .
[A] //                          .   . .   .
[A] //                         7 --- 8 --- 9
[A] //                        . .   . .   . .
[A] //                       .   . .   . .   .
[A] //                      3 --- 4 --- 5 --- 6
[A] //                         0 --- 1 --- 2
[A] //  The equivalant quartic Bezier triangle (15 points):
[A] //                              14
[A] //                              . .
[A] //                             .   .
[A] //                           12 --- 13
[A] //                         9 -- 10 --- 11
[A] //                      5 --- 6 --- 7 --- 8
[A] //                     . .   . .   . .   . .
[A] //                    .   . .   . .   . .   .
[A] //                   0 --- 1 --- 2 --- 3 --- 4
[A] //  A hybrid cubic/quartic Bezier patch with cubic boundaries is a close
[A] //  approximation and would only use 12 control points, but we need a full
[A] //  quartic patch to maintain accuracy along boundary curves -- especially
[A] //  between subdivision levels.
[A] OsdComputePerPatchVertexBoxSplineTriangle(ivec3 patchParam, int ID, vec3 cv[12],
[A] //  Conversion matrix from 12-point Box spline to 15-point quartic Bezier
[A] //  patch and its common scale factor:
[A] const float boxToBezierMatrix[12*15] = float[12*15](
[A] // L0   L1   L2     L3   L4   L5   L6     L7   L8   L9     L10  L11
[A] 2,   2,   0,     2,  12,   2,   0,     2,   2,   0,     0,   0,  // B0
[A] 1,   3,   0,     0,  12,   4,   0,     1,   3,   0,     0,   0,  // B1
[A] 0,   4,   0,     0,   8,   8,   0,     0,   4,   0,     0,   0,  // B2
[A] 0,   3,   1,     0,   4,  12,   0,     0,   3,   1,     0,   0,  // B3
[A] 0,   2,   2,     0,   2,  12,   2,     0,   2,   2,     0,   0,  // B4
[A] 0,   1,   0,     1,  12,   3,   0,     3,   4,   0,     0,   0,  // B5
[A] 0,   1,   0,     0,  10,   6,   0,     1,   6,   0,     0,   0,  // B6
[A] 0,   1,   0,     0,   6,  10,   0,     0,   6,   1,     0,   0,  // B7
[A] 0,   1,   0,     0,   3,  12,   1,     0,   4,   3,     0,   0,  // B8
[A] 0,   0,   0,     0,   8,   4,   0,     4,   8,   0,     0,   0,  // B9
[A] 0,   0,   0,     0,   6,   6,   0,     1,  10,   1,     0,   0,  // B10
[A] 0,   0,   0,     0,   4,   8,   0,     0,   8,   4,     0,   0,  // B11
[A] 0,   0,   0,     0,   4,   3,   0,     3,  12,   1,     1,   0,  // B12
[A] 0,   0,   0,     0,   3,   4,   0,     1,  12,   3,     0,   1,  // B13
[A] 0,   0,   0,     0,   2,   2,   0,     2,  12,   2,     2,   2   // B14
[A] const float boxToBezierMatrixScale = 1.0 / 24.0;
[A] OsdComputeBoxSplineTriangleBoundaryPoints(cv, patchParam);
[A] int cvCoeffBase = 12 * ID;
[A] for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) {
[A] result.P += boxToBezierMatrix[cvCoeffBase + i] * cv[i];
[A] result.P *= boxToBezierMatrixScale;
[A] OsdEvalPatchBezierTriangle(ivec3 patchParam, vec2 UV,
[A] OsdPerPatchVertexBezier cv[15],
[A] float u = UV.x;
[A] float v = UV.y;
[A] float w = 1.0 - u - v;
[A] float uu = u * u;
[A] float vv = v * v;
[A] float ww = w * w;
[A] #ifdef OSD_COMPUTE_NORMAL_DERIVATIVES
[A] //  When computing normal derivatives, we need 2nd derivatives, so compute
[A] //  an intermediate quadratic Bezier triangle from which 2nd derivatives
[A] //  can be easily computed, and which in turn yields the triangle that gives
[A] //  the position and 1st derivatives.
[A] //  Quadratic barycentric basis functions (in addition to those above):
[A] float uv = u * v * 2.0;
[A] float vw = v * w * 2.0;
[A] float wu = w * u * 2.0;
[A] vec3 Q0 = ww * cv[ 0].P + wu * cv[ 1].P + uu * cv[ 2].P +
[A] uv * cv[ 6].P + vv * cv[ 9].P + vw * cv[ 5].P;
[A] vec3 Q1 = ww * cv[ 1].P + wu * cv[ 2].P + uu * cv[ 3].P +
[A] uv * cv[ 7].P + vv * cv[10].P + vw * cv[ 6].P;
[A] vec3 Q2 = ww * cv[ 2].P + wu * cv[ 3].P + uu * cv[ 4].P +
[A] uv * cv[ 8].P + vv * cv[11].P + vw * cv[ 7].P;
[A] vec3 Q3 = ww * cv[ 5].P + wu * cv[ 6].P + uu * cv[ 7].P +
[A] uv * cv[10].P + vv * cv[12].P + vw * cv[ 9].P;
[A] vec3 Q4 = ww * cv[ 6].P + wu * cv[ 7].P + uu * cv[ 8].P +
[A] uv * cv[11].P + vv * cv[13].P + vw * cv[10].P;
[A] vec3 Q5 = ww * cv[ 9].P + wu * cv[10].P + uu * cv[11].P +
[A] uv * cv[13].P + vv * cv[14].P + vw * cv[12].P;
[A] vec3 V0 = w * Q0 + u * Q1 + v * Q3;
[A] vec3 V1 = w * Q1 + u * Q2 + v * Q4;
[A] vec3 V2 = w * Q3 + u * Q4 + v * Q5;
[A] //  When 2nd derivatives are not required, factor the recursive evaluation
[A] //  of a point to directly provide the three points of the triangle at the
[A] //  last stage -- which then trivially provides both position and 1st
[A] //  derivatives.  Each point of the triangle results from evaluating the
[A] //  corresponding cubic Bezier sub-triangle for each corner of the quartic:
[A] //  Cubic barycentric basis functions:
[A] float uuu = uu * u;
[A] float uuv = uu * v * 3.0;
[A] float uvv = u * vv * 3.0;
[A] float vvv = vv * v;
[A] float vvw = vv * w * 3.0;
[A] float vww = v * ww * 3.0;
[A] float www = ww * w;
[A] float wwu = ww * u * 3.0;
[A] float wuu = w * uu * 3.0;
[A] float uvw = u * v * w * 6.0;
[A] vec3 V0 = www * cv[ 0].P + wwu * cv[ 1].P + wuu * cv[ 2].P
[A] + uuu * cv[ 3].P + uuv * cv[ 7].P + uvv * cv[10].P
[A] + vvv * cv[12].P + vvw * cv[ 9].P + vww * cv[ 5].P + uvw * cv[ 6].P;
[A] vec3 V1 = www * cv[ 1].P + wwu * cv[ 2].P + wuu * cv[ 3].P
[A] + uuu * cv[ 4].P + uuv * cv[ 8].P + uvv * cv[11].P
[A] + vvv * cv[13].P + vvw * cv[10].P + vww * cv[ 6].P + uvw * cv[ 7].P;
[A] vec3 V2 = www * cv[ 5].P + wwu * cv[ 6].P + wuu * cv[ 7].P
[A] + uuu * cv[ 8].P + uuv * cv[11].P + uvv * cv[13].P
[A] + vvv * cv[14].P + vvw * cv[12].P + vww * cv[ 9].P + uvw * cv[10].P;
[A] //  Compute P, du and dv all from the triangle formed from the three Vi:
[A] P = w * V0 + u * V1 + v * V2;
[A] int dSign = OsdGetPatchIsTriangleRotated(patchParam) ? -1 : 1;
[A] float d1Scale = dSign * level * 4;
[A] dPu = (V1 - V0) * d1Scale;
[A] dPv = (V2 - V0) * d1Scale;
[A] //  Compute N and test for degeneracy:
[A] //  Compute normal derivatives using 2nd order partials, then use the
[A] //  normal derivatives to resolve a degenerate normal:
[A] float d2Scale = dSign * level * level * 12;
[A] vec3 dUU = (Q0 - 2 * Q1 + Q2)  * d2Scale;
[A] vec3 dVV = (Q0 - 2 * Q3 + Q5)  * d2Scale;
[A] vec3 dUV = (Q0 - Q1 + Q4 - Q3) * d2Scale;
[A] if (nLength < nEpsilon) {
[A] //  Use 1st order Taylor approximation of N(u,v) within patch interior:
[A] if (w > 0.0) {
[A] N =  dNu + dNv;
[A] } else if (u >= 1.0) {
[A] N = -dNu + dNv;
[A] } else if (v >= 1.0) {
[A] N =  dNu - dNv;
[A] N = -dNu - dNv;
[A] nLength = 1.0;
[A] //  Project derivs of non-unit normal function onto tangent plane of N:
[A] dNu = (dNu - dot(dNu,N) * N) / nLength;
[A] dNv = (dNv - dot(dNv,N) * N) / nLength;
[A] //  Resolve a degenerate normal using the interior triangle of the
[A] //  intermediate quadratic patch that results from recursive evaluation.
[A] //  This addresses common cases of degenerate or colinear boundaries
[A] //  without resorting to use of explicit 2nd derivatives:
[A] float uv  = u * v * 2.0;
[A] float vw  = v * w * 2.0;
[A] float wu  = w * u * 2.0;
[A] N = cross((Q4 - Q1), (Q3 - Q1));
[A] OsdEvalPatchGregoryTriangle(ivec3 patchParam, vec2 UV, vec3 cv[18],
[A] float duv = u + v;
[A] float dvw = v + w;
[A] float dwu = w + u;
[A] OsdPerPatchVertexBezier bezcv[15];
[A] bezcv[ 6].P = (duv == 0.0) ? cv[3]  : ((u*cv[ 3] + v*cv[ 4]) / duv);
[A] bezcv[ 7].P = (dvw == 0.0) ? cv[8]  : ((v*cv[ 8] + w*cv[ 9]) / dvw);
[A] bezcv[10].P = (dwu == 0.0) ? cv[13] : ((w*cv[13] + u*cv[14]) / dwu);
[A] bezcv[ 0].P = cv[ 0];
[A] bezcv[ 1].P = cv[ 1];
[A] bezcv[ 2].P = cv[15];
[A] bezcv[ 3].P = cv[ 7];
[A] bezcv[ 4].P = cv[ 5];
[A] bezcv[ 5].P = cv[ 2];
[A] bezcv[ 8].P = cv[ 6];
[A] bezcv[ 9].P = cv[17];
[A] bezcv[11].P = cv[16];
[A] bezcv[12].P = cv[11];
[A] bezcv[13].P = cv[12];
[A] bezcv[14].P = cv[10];
[A] OsdEvalPatchBezierTriangle(patchParam, UV, bezcv, P, dPu, dPv, N, dNu, dNv);
[A] //   Copyright 2013-2018 Pixar
[A] // Tessellation
[A] // For now, fractional spacing is supported only with screen space tessellation
[A] #ifndef OSD_ENABLE_SCREENSPACE_TESSELLATION
[A] #undef OSD_FRACTIONAL_EVEN_SPACING
[A] #undef OSD_FRACTIONAL_ODD_SPACING
[A] #if defined OSD_FRACTIONAL_EVEN_SPACING
[A] #define OSD_SPACING fractional_even_spacing
[A] #elif defined OSD_FRACTIONAL_ODD_SPACING
[A] #define OSD_SPACING fractional_odd_spacing
[A] #define OSD_SPACING equal_spacing
[A] // Organization of B-spline and Bezier control points.
[A] // Each patch is defined by 16 control points (labeled 0-15).
[A] // The patch will be evaluated across the domain from (0,0) at
[A] // the lower-left to (1,1) at the upper-right. When computing
[A] // adaptive tessellation metrics, we consider refined vertex-vertex
[A] // and edge-vertex points along the transition edges of the patch
[A] // (labeled vv* and ev* respectively).
[A] // The two segments of each transition edge are labeled Lo and Hi,
[A] // with the Lo segment occurring before the Hi segment along the
[A] // transition edge's domain parameterization. These Lo and Hi segment
[A] // tessellation levels determine how domain evaluation coordinates
[A] // are remapped along transition edges. The Hi segment value will
[A] // be zero for a non-transition edge.
[A] // (0,1)                                         (1,1)
[A] //   vv3                  ev23                   vv2
[A] //        |       Lo3       |       Hi3       |
[A] //      --O-----------O-----+-----O-----------O--
[A] //        | 12        | 13     14 |        15 |
[A] //        |           |           |           |
[A] //    Hi0 |           |           |           | Hi2
[A] //        O-----------O-----------O-----------O
[A] //        | 8         | 9      10 |        11 |
[A] // ev03 --+           |           |           +-- ev12
[A] //        | 4         | 5       6 |         7 |
[A] //    Lo0 |           |           |           | Lo2
[A] //        | 0         | 1       2 |         3 |
[A] //        |       Lo1       |       Hi1       |
[A] //   vv0                  ev01                   vv1
[A] // (0,0)                                         (1,0)
[A] #define OSD_MAX_TESS_LEVEL gl_MaxTessGenLevel
[A] float OsdComputePostProjectionSphereExtent(vec3 center, float diameter)
[A] vec4 p = OsdProjectionMatrix() * vec4(center, 1.0);
[A] return abs(diameter * OsdProjectionMatrix()[1][1] / p.w);
[A] float OsdComputeTessLevel(vec3 p0, vec3 p1)
[A] // Adaptive factor can be any computation that depends only on arg values.
[A] // Project the diameter of the edge's bounding sphere instead of using the
[A] // length of the projected edge itself to avoid problems near silhouettes.
[A] p0 = (OsdModelViewMatrix() * vec4(p0, 1.0)).xyz;
[A] p1 = (OsdModelViewMatrix() * vec4(p1, 1.0)).xyz;
[A] vec3 center = (p0 + p1) / 2.0;
[A] float diameter = distance(p0, p1);
[A] float projLength = OsdComputePostProjectionSphereExtent(center, diameter);
[A] float tessLevel = max(1.0, OsdTessLevel() * projLength);
[A] // We restrict adaptive tessellation levels to half of the device
[A] // supported maximum because transition edges are split into two
[A] // halves and the sum of the two corresponding levels must not exceed
[A] // the device maximum. We impose this limit even for non-transition
[A] // edges because a non-transition edge must be able to match up with
[A] // one half of the transition edge of an adjacent transition patch.
[A] return min(tessLevel, OSD_MAX_TESS_LEVEL / 2);
[A] OsdGetTessLevelsUniform(ivec3 patchParam,
[A] out vec4 tessOuterLo, out vec4 tessOuterHi)
[A] // Uniform factors are simple powers of two for each level.
[A] // The maximum here can be increased if we know the maximum
[A] // refinement level of the mesh:
[A] //     min(OSD_MAX_TESS_LEVEL, pow(2, MaximumRefinementLevel-1)
[A] int refinementLevel = OsdGetPatchRefinementLevel(patchParam);
[A] float tessLevel = min(OsdTessLevel(), OSD_MAX_TESS_LEVEL) /
[A] pow(2, refinementLevel-1);
[A] // tessLevels of transition edge should be clamped to 2.
[A] int transitionMask = OsdGetPatchTransitionMask(patchParam);
[A] vec4 tessLevelMin = vec4(1) + vec4(((transitionMask & 8) >> 3),
[A] ((transitionMask & 1) >> 0),
[A] ((transitionMask & 2) >> 1),
[A] ((transitionMask & 4) >> 2));
[A] tessOuterLo = max(vec4(tessLevel), tessLevelMin);
[A] tessOuterHi = vec4(0);
[A] OsdGetTessLevelsUniformTriangle(ivec3 patchParam,
[A] vec4 tessLevelMin = vec4(1) + vec4(((transitionMask & 4) >> 2),
[A] 0);
[A] OsdGetTessLevelsRefinedPoints(vec3 cp[16], ivec3 patchParam,
[A] // Each edge of a transition patch is adjacent to one or two patches
[A] // at the next refined level of subdivision. We compute the corresponding
[A] // vertex-vertex and edge-vertex refined points along the edges of the
[A] // patch using Catmull-Clark subdivision stencil weights.
[A] // For simplicity, we let the optimizer discard unused computation.
[A] vec3 vv0 = (cp[0] + cp[2] + cp[8] + cp[10]) * 0.015625 +
[A] (cp[1] + cp[4] + cp[6] + cp[9]) * 0.09375 + cp[5] * 0.5625;
[A] vec3 ev01 = (cp[1] + cp[2] + cp[9] + cp[10]) * 0.0625 +
[A] (cp[5] + cp[6]) * 0.375;
[A] vec3 vv1 = (cp[1] + cp[3] + cp[9] + cp[11]) * 0.015625 +
[A] (cp[2] + cp[5] + cp[7] + cp[10]) * 0.09375 + cp[6] * 0.5625;
[A] vec3 ev12 = (cp[5] + cp[7] + cp[9] + cp[11]) * 0.0625 +
[A] (cp[6] + cp[10]) * 0.375;
[A] vec3 vv2 = (cp[5] + cp[7] + cp[13] + cp[15]) * 0.015625 +
[A] (cp[6] + cp[9] + cp[11] + cp[14]) * 0.09375 + cp[10] * 0.5625;
[A] vec3 ev23 = (cp[5] + cp[6] + cp[13] + cp[14]) * 0.0625 +
[A] (cp[9] + cp[10]) * 0.375;
[A] vec3 vv3 = (cp[4] + cp[6] + cp[12] + cp[14]) * 0.015625 +
[A] (cp[5] + cp[8] + cp[10] + cp[13]) * 0.09375 + cp[9] * 0.5625;
[A] vec3 ev03 = (cp[4] + cp[6] + cp[8] + cp[10]) * 0.0625 +
[A] (cp[5] + cp[9]) * 0.375;
[A] tessOuterLo = vec4(0);
[A] if ((transitionMask & 8) != 0) {
[A] tessOuterLo[0] = OsdComputeTessLevel(vv0, ev03);
[A] tessOuterHi[0] = OsdComputeTessLevel(vv3, ev03);
[A] tessOuterLo[0] = OsdComputeTessLevel(cp[5], cp[9]);
[A] if ((transitionMask & 1) != 0) {
[A] tessOuterLo[1] = OsdComputeTessLevel(vv0, ev01);
[A] tessOuterHi[1] = OsdComputeTessLevel(vv1, ev01);
[A] tessOuterLo[1] = OsdComputeTessLevel(cp[5], cp[6]);
[A] if ((transitionMask & 2) != 0) {
[A] tessOuterLo[2] = OsdComputeTessLevel(vv1, ev12);
[A] tessOuterHi[2] = OsdComputeTessLevel(vv2, ev12);
[A] tessOuterLo[2] = OsdComputeTessLevel(cp[6], cp[10]);
[A] if ((transitionMask & 4) != 0) {
[A] tessOuterLo[3] = OsdComputeTessLevel(vv3, ev23);
[A] tessOuterHi[3] = OsdComputeTessLevel(vv2, ev23);
[A] tessOuterLo[3] = OsdComputeTessLevel(cp[9], cp[10]);
[A] //  Patch boundary corners are ordered counter-clockwise from the first
[A] //  corner while patch boundary edges and their midpoints are similarly
[A] //  ordered counter-clockwise beginning at the edge preceding corner[0].
[A] Osd_GetTessLevelsFromPatchBoundaries4(vec3 corners[4], vec3 midpoints[4],
[A] ivec3 patchParam, out vec4 tessOuterLo, out vec4 tessOuterHi)
[A] tessOuterLo[0] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[0], midpoints[0]);
[A] tessOuterHi[0] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[3], midpoints[0]);
[A] tessOuterLo[0] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[0], corners[3]);
[A] tessOuterLo[1] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[0], midpoints[1]);
[A] tessOuterHi[1] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[1], midpoints[1]);
[A] tessOuterLo[1] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[0], corners[1]);
[A] tessOuterLo[2] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[1], midpoints[2]);
[A] tessOuterHi[2] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[2], midpoints[2]);
[A] tessOuterLo[2] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[1], corners[2]);
[A] tessOuterLo[3] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[3], midpoints[3]);
[A] tessOuterHi[3] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[2], midpoints[3]);
[A] tessOuterLo[3] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[3], corners[2]);
[A] Osd_GetTessLevelsFromPatchBoundaries3(vec3 corners[3], vec3 midpoints[3],
[A] tessOuterHi[0] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[2], midpoints[0]);
[A] tessOuterLo[0] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[0], corners[2]);
[A] tessOuterLo[2] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[2], midpoints[2]);
[A] tessOuterHi[2] = OsdComputeTessLevel(corners[1], midpoints[2]);
[A] Osd_EvalBezierCurveMidPoint(vec3 p0, vec3 p1, vec3 p2, vec3 p3)
[A] //  Coefficients for the midpoint are { 1/8, 3/8, 3/8, 1/8 }:
[A] return 0.125 * (p0 + p3) + 0.375 * (p1 + p2);
[A] Osd_EvalQuarticBezierCurveMidPoint(vec3 p0, vec3 p1, vec3 p2, vec3 p3, vec3 p4)
[A] //  Coefficients for the midpoint are { 1/16, 1/4, 3/8, 1/4, 1/16 }:
[A] return 0.0625 * (p0 + p4) + 0.25 * (p1 + p3) + 0.375 * p2;
[A] OsdEvalPatchBezierTessLevels(OsdPerPatchVertexBezier cpBezier[16],
[A] // at the next refined level of subdivision. When the patch control
[A] // points have been converted to the Bezier basis, the control points
[A] // at the four corners are on the limit surface (since a Bezier patch
[A] // interpolates its corner control points). We can compute an adaptive
[A] // tessellation level for transition edges on the limit surface by
[A] // evaluating a limit position at the mid point of each transition edge.
[A] vec3 corners[4];
[A] vec3 midpoints[4];
[A] corners[0] = OsdEvalBezier(cpBezier, patchParam, vec2(0.0, 0.0));
[A] corners[1] = OsdEvalBezier(cpBezier, patchParam, vec2(1.0, 0.0));
[A] corners[2] = OsdEvalBezier(cpBezier, patchParam, vec2(1.0, 1.0));
[A] corners[3] = OsdEvalBezier(cpBezier, patchParam, vec2(0.0, 1.0));
[A] midpoints[0] = ((transitionMask & 8) == 0) ? vec3(0) :
[A] OsdEvalBezier(cpBezier, patchParam, vec2(0.0, 0.5));
[A] midpoints[1] = ((transitionMask & 1) == 0) ? vec3(0) :
[A] OsdEvalBezier(cpBezier, patchParam, vec2(0.5, 0.0));
[A] midpoints[2] = ((transitionMask & 2) == 0) ? vec3(0) :
[A] OsdEvalBezier(cpBezier, patchParam, vec2(1.0, 0.5));
[A] midpoints[3] = ((transitionMask & 4) == 0) ? vec3(0) :
[A] OsdEvalBezier(cpBezier, patchParam, vec2(0.5, 1.0));
[A] corners[0] = cpBezier[ 0].P;
[A] corners[1] = cpBezier[ 3].P;
[A] corners[2] = cpBezier[15].P;
[A] corners[3] = cpBezier[12].P;
[A] Osd_EvalBezierCurveMidPoint(
[A] cpBezier[0].P, cpBezier[4].P, cpBezier[8].P, cpBezier[12].P);
[A] cpBezier[0].P, cpBezier[1].P, cpBezier[2].P, cpBezier[3].P);
[A] cpBezier[3].P, cpBezier[7].P, cpBezier[11].P, cpBezier[15].P);
[A] cpBezier[12].P, cpBezier[13].P, cpBezier[14].P, cpBezier[15].P);
[A] Osd_GetTessLevelsFromPatchBoundaries4(corners, midpoints,
[A] patchParam, tessOuterLo, tessOuterHi);
[A] OsdEvalPatchBezierTriangleTessLevels(vec3 cv[15],
[A] // at the corners are on the limit surface (since a Bezier patch
[A] vec3 corners[3];
[A] corners[0] = cv[0];
[A] corners[1] = cv[4];
[A] corners[2] = cv[14];
[A] vec3 midpoints[3];
[A] midpoints[0] = ((transitionMask & 4) == 0) ? vec3(0) :
[A] Osd_EvalQuarticBezierCurveMidPoint(cv[0], cv[5], cv[9], cv[12], cv[14]);
[A] Osd_EvalQuarticBezierCurveMidPoint(cv[0], cv[1], cv[2], cv[3], cv[4]);
[A] Osd_EvalQuarticBezierCurveMidPoint(cv[4], cv[8], cv[11], cv[13], cv[14]);
[A] Osd_GetTessLevelsFromPatchBoundaries3(corners, midpoints,
[A] // Round up to the nearest even integer
[A] float OsdRoundUpEven(float x) {
[A] return 2*ceil(x/2);
[A] // Round up to the nearest odd integer
[A] float OsdRoundUpOdd(float x) {
[A] return 2*ceil((x+1)/2)-1;
[A] // Compute outer and inner tessellation levels taking into account the
[A] // current tessellation spacing mode.
[A] OsdComputeTessLevels(inout vec4 tessOuterLo, inout vec4 tessOuterHi,
[A] out vec4 tessLevelOuter, out vec2 tessLevelInner)
[A] // Outer levels are the sum of the Lo and Hi segments where the Hi
[A] // segments will have lengths of zero for non-transition edges.
[A] // Combine fractional outer transition edge levels before rounding.
[A] vec4 combinedOuter = tessOuterLo + tessOuterHi;
[A] // Round the segments of transition edges separately. We will recover the
[A] // fractional parameterization of transition edges after tessellation.
[A] tessLevelOuter = combinedOuter;
[A] if (tessOuterHi[0] > 0) {
[A] tessLevelOuter[0] =
[A] OsdRoundUpEven(tessOuterLo[0]) + OsdRoundUpEven(tessOuterHi[0]);
[A] if (tessOuterHi[1] > 0) {
[A] tessLevelOuter[1] =
[A] OsdRoundUpEven(tessOuterLo[1]) + OsdRoundUpEven(tessOuterHi[1]);
[A] if (tessOuterHi[2] > 0) {
[A] tessLevelOuter[2] =
[A] OsdRoundUpEven(tessOuterLo[2]) + OsdRoundUpEven(tessOuterHi[2]);
[A] if (tessOuterHi[3] > 0) {
[A] tessLevelOuter[3] =
[A] OsdRoundUpEven(tessOuterLo[3]) + OsdRoundUpEven(tessOuterHi[3]);
[A] // The sum of the two outer odd segment lengths will be an even number
[A] // which the tessellator will increase by +1 so that there will be a
[A] // total odd number of segments. We clamp the combinedOuter tess levels
[A] // (used to compute the inner tess levels) so that the outer transition
[A] // edges will be sampled without degenerate triangles.
[A] OsdRoundUpOdd(tessOuterLo[0]) + OsdRoundUpOdd(tessOuterHi[0]);
[A] combinedOuter = max(vec4(3), combinedOuter);
[A] OsdRoundUpOdd(tessOuterLo[1]) + OsdRoundUpOdd(tessOuterHi[1]);
[A] OsdRoundUpOdd(tessOuterLo[2]) + OsdRoundUpOdd(tessOuterHi[2]);
[A] OsdRoundUpOdd(tessOuterLo[3]) + OsdRoundUpOdd(tessOuterHi[3]);
[A] // Round equally spaced transition edge levels before combining.
[A] tessOuterLo = round(tessOuterLo);
[A] tessOuterHi = round(tessOuterHi);
[A] // Inner levels are the averages the corresponding outer levels.
[A] tessLevelInner[0] = (combinedOuter[1] + combinedOuter[3]) * 0.5;
[A] tessLevelInner[1] = (combinedOuter[0] + combinedOuter[2]) * 0.5;
[A] OsdComputeTessLevelsTriangle(inout vec4 tessOuterLo, inout vec4 tessOuterHi,
[A] OsdComputeTessLevels(tessOuterLo, tessOuterHi,
[A] tessLevelOuter, tessLevelInner);
[A] // Inner level is the max of the three outer levels.
[A] tessLevelInner[0] = max(max(tessLevelOuter[0],
[A] tessLevelOuter[1]),
[A] tessLevelOuter[2]);
[A] out vec4 tessLevelOuter, out vec2 tessLevelInner,
[A] OsdGetTessLevelsUniform(patchParam, tessOuterLo, tessOuterHi);
[A] OsdGetTessLevelsUniformTriangle(patchParam, tessOuterLo, tessOuterHi);

[A] OsdGetTessLevelsAdaptiveLimitPoints(OsdPerPatchVertexBezier cpBezier[16],

[A] vec4 tessOuterLo = vec4(0);
[A] vec4 tessOuterHi = vec4(0);

[A] tessOuterLo[0] = OsdComputeTessLevel(cp0, cp1);
[A] tessOuterLo[1] = OsdComputeTessLevel(cp0, cp3);
[A] tessOuterLo[2] = OsdComputeTessLevel(cp2, cp3);

[A] #if defined OSD_FRACTIONAL_EVEN_SPACING || defined OSD_FRACTIONAL_ODD_SPACING
[A] OsdGetTessFractionalSplit(float t, float level, float levelUp)
[A] // Fractional tessellation of an edge will produce n segments where n
[A] // is the tessellation level of the edge (level) rounded up to the
[A] // nearest even or odd integer (levelUp). There will be n-2 segments of
[A] // equal length (dx1) and two additional segments of equal length (dx0)
[A] // that are typically shorter than the other segments. The two additional
[A] // segments should be placed symmetrically on opposite sides of the
[A] // edge (offset).
[A] if (level <= 2) return t;
[A] float base = pow(2.0,floor(log2(levelUp)));
[A] float offset = 1.0/(int(2*base-levelUp)/2 & int(base/2-1));
[A] if (level <= 1) return t;
[A] float offset = 1.0/(((int(2*base-levelUp)/2+1) & int(base/2-1))+1);
[A] float dx0 = (1.0 - (levelUp-level)/2) / levelUp;
[A] float dx1 = (1.0 - 2.0*dx0) / (levelUp - 2.0*ceil(dx0));
[A] if (t < 0.5) {
[A] float x = levelUp/2 - round(t*levelUp);
[A] return 0.5 - (x*dx1 + int(x*offset > 1) * (dx0 - dx1));
[A] } else if (t > 0.5) {
[A] float x = round(t*levelUp) - levelUp/2;
[A] return 0.5 + (x*dx1 + int(x*offset > 1) * (dx0 - dx1));
[A] return t;
[A] OsdGetTessTransitionSplit(float t, float lo, float hi)
[A] float loRoundUp = OsdRoundUpEven(lo);
[A] float hiRoundUp = OsdRoundUpEven(hi);
[A] // Convert the parametric t into a segment index along the combined edge.
[A] float ti = round(t * (loRoundUp + hiRoundUp));
[A] if (ti <= loRoundUp) {
[A] float t0 = ti / loRoundUp;
[A] return OsdGetTessFractionalSplit(t0, lo, loRoundUp) * 0.5;
[A] float t1 = (ti - loRoundUp) / hiRoundUp;
[A] return OsdGetTessFractionalSplit(t1, hi, hiRoundUp) * 0.5 + 0.5;
[A] float loRoundUp = OsdRoundUpOdd(lo);
[A] float hiRoundUp = OsdRoundUpOdd(hi);
[A] // The +1 below is to account for the extra segment produced by the
[A] // tessellator since the sum of two odd tess levels will be rounded
[A] // up by one to the next odd integer tess level.
[A] float ti = round(t * (loRoundUp + hiRoundUp + 1));
[A] } else if (ti > (loRoundUp+1)) {
[A] float t1 = (ti - (loRoundUp+1)) / hiRoundUp;
[A] return 0.5;
[A] float ti = round(t * (lo + hi));
[A] if (ti <= lo) {
[A] return (ti / lo) * 0.5;
[A] return ((ti - lo) / hi) * 0.5 + 0.5;
[A] OsdGetTessParameterization(vec2 p, vec4 tessOuterLo, vec4 tessOuterHi)
[A] vec2 UV = p;
[A] if (p.x == 0 && tessOuterHi[0] > 0) {
[A] UV.y = OsdGetTessTransitionSplit(UV.y, tessOuterLo[0], tessOuterHi[0]);
[A] } else
[A] if (p.y == 0 && tessOuterHi[1] > 0) {
[A] UV.x = OsdGetTessTransitionSplit(UV.x, tessOuterLo[1], tessOuterHi[1]);
[A] if (p.x == 1 && tessOuterHi[2] > 0) {
[A] UV.y = OsdGetTessTransitionSplit(UV.y, tessOuterLo[2], tessOuterHi[2]);
[A] if (p.y == 1 && tessOuterHi[3] > 0) {
[A] UV.x = OsdGetTessTransitionSplit(UV.x, tessOuterLo[3], tessOuterHi[3]);
[A] return UV;
[A] OsdGetTessParameterizationTriangle(vec3 p, vec4 tessOuterLo, vec4 tessOuterHi)
[A] vec2 UV = p.xy;
[A] if (p.z == 0 && tessOuterHi[2] > 0) {
[A] UV.x = OsdGetTessTransitionSplit(UV.x, tessOuterLo[2], tessOuterHi[2]);
[A] UV.y = 1.0 - UV.x;
[A] // Legacy Gregory
[A] #if defined(OSD_PATCH_GREGORY) || defined(OSD_PATCH_GREGORY_BOUNDARY)
[A] #define M_PI 3.14159265359f
[A] // precomputed catmark coefficient table up to valence 29
[A] uniform float OsdCatmarkCoefficient[30] = float[](
[A] 0, 0, 0, 0.812816, 0.500000, 0.363644, 0.287514,
[A] 0.238688, 0.204544, 0.179229, 0.159657,
[A] 0.144042, 0.131276, 0.120632, 0.111614,
[A] 0.103872, 0.09715, 0.0912559, 0.0860444,
[A] 0.0814022, 0.0772401, 0.0734867, 0.0700842,
[A] 0.0669851, 0.0641504, 0.0615475, 0.0591488,
[A] 0.0569311, 0.0548745, 0.0529621
[A] OsdComputeCatmarkCoefficient(int valence)
[A] #if OSD_MAX_VALENCE < 30
[A] return OsdCatmarkCoefficient[valence];
[A] if (valence < 30) {
[A] float t = 2.0f * float(M_PI) / float(valence);
[A] return 1.0f / (valence * (cos(t) + 5.0f +
[A] sqrt((cos(t) + 9) * (cos(t) + 1)))/16.0f);
[A] float cosfn(int n, int j) {
[A] return cos((2.0f * M_PI * j)/float(n));
[A] float sinfn(int n, int j) {
[A] return sin((2.0f * M_PI * j)/float(n));
[A] #if !defined OSD_MAX_VALENCE || OSD_MAX_VALENCE < 1
[A] #undef OSD_MAX_VALENCE
[A] #define OSD_MAX_VALENCE 4

[A] struct OsdPerPatchVertexGregory {
[A] vec3 Ep;

[A] vec3 Fp;
[A] vec3 Fm;
[A] #ifndef OSD_NUM_ELEMENTS
[A] #define OSD_NUM_ELEMENTS 3
[A] uniform samplerBuffer OsdVertexBuffer;
[A] uniform isamplerBuffer OsdValenceBuffer;
[A] vec3 OsdReadVertex(int vertexIndex)
[A] int index = int(OSD_NUM_ELEMENTS * (vertexIndex + OsdBaseVertex()));
[A] return vec3(texelFetch(OsdVertexBuffer, index).x,
[A] texelFetch(OsdVertexBuffer, index+1).x,
[A] texelFetch(OsdVertexBuffer, index+2).x);
[A] int OsdReadVertexValence(int vertexID)
[A] int index = int(vertexID * (2 * OSD_MAX_VALENCE + 1));
[A] return texelFetch(OsdValenceBuffer, index).x;
[A] int OsdReadVertexIndex(int vertexID, int valenceVertex)
[A] int index = int(vertexID * (2 * OSD_MAX_VALENCE + 1) + 1 + valenceVertex);
[A] uniform isamplerBuffer OsdQuadOffsetBuffer;
[A] int OsdReadQuadOffset(int primitiveID, int offsetVertex)
[A] int index = int(4*primitiveID+OsdGregoryQuadOffsetBase() + offsetVertex);
[A] return texelFetch(OsdQuadOffsetBuffer, index).x;
[A] OsdComputePerVertexGregory(int vID, vec3 P, out OsdPerVertexGregory v)
[A] v.clipFlag = ivec3(0);
[A] int ivalence = OsdReadVertexValence(vID);
[A] v.valence = ivalence;
[A] int valence = abs(ivalence);
[A] vec3 f[OSD_MAX_VALENCE];
[A] vec3 pos = P;
[A] vec3 opos = vec3(0);
[A] v.org = pos;
[A] int boundaryEdgeNeighbors[2];
[A] int currNeighbor = 0;
[A] int ibefore = 0;
[A] int zerothNeighbor = 0;
[A] for (int i=0; i<valence; ++i) {
[A] int im = (i+valence-1)%valence;
[A] int ip = (i+1)%valence;
[A] int idx_neighbor = OsdReadVertexIndex(vID, 2*i);
[A] bool isBoundaryNeighbor = false;
[A] int valenceNeighbor = OsdReadVertexValence(idx_neighbor);
[A] if (valenceNeighbor < 0) {
[A] isBoundaryNeighbor = true;
[A] if (currNeighbor<2) {
[A] boundaryEdgeNeighbors[currNeighbor] = idx_neighbor;
[A] currNeighbor++;
[A] if (currNeighbor == 1) {
[A] ibefore = i;
[A] zerothNeighbor = i;
[A] if (i-ibefore == 1) {
[A] int tmp = boundaryEdgeNeighbors[0];
[A] boundaryEdgeNeighbors[0] = boundaryEdgeNeighbors[1];
[A] boundaryEdgeNeighbors[1] = tmp;
[A] vec3 neighbor = OsdReadVertex(idx_neighbor);
[A] int idx_diagonal = OsdReadVertexIndex(vID, 2*i + 1);
[A] vec3 diagonal = OsdReadVertex(idx_diagonal);
[A] int idx_neighbor_p = OsdReadVertexIndex(vID, 2*ip);
[A] vec3 neighbor_p = OsdReadVertex(idx_neighbor_p);
[A] int idx_neighbor_m = OsdReadVertexIndex(vID, 2*im);
[A] vec3 neighbor_m = OsdReadVertex(idx_neighbor_m);
[A] int idx_diagonal_m = OsdReadVertexIndex(vID, 2*im + 1);
[A] vec3 diagonal_m = OsdReadVertex(idx_diagonal_m);
[A] f[i] = (pos * float(valence) + (neighbor_p + neighbor)*2.0f + diagonal) / (float(valence)+5.0f);
[A] opos += f[i];
[A] v.r[i] = (neighbor_p-neighbor_m)/3.0f + (diagonal - diagonal_m)/6.0f;
[A] opos /= valence;
[A] v.P = vec4(opos, 1.0f).xyz;
[A] vec3 e;
[A] v.e0 = vec3(0);
[A] v.e1 = vec3(0);
[A] for(int i=0; i<valence; ++i) {
[A] int im = (i + valence -1) % valence;
[A] e = 0.5f * (f[i] + f[im]);
[A] v.e0 += cosfn(valence, i)*e;
[A] v.e1 += sinfn(valence, i)*e;
[A] float ef = OsdComputeCatmarkCoefficient(valence);
[A] v.e0 *= ef;
[A] v.e1 *= ef;
[A] v.zerothNeighbor = zerothNeighbor;
[A] boundaryEdgeNeighbors[1] = boundaryEdgeNeighbors[0];
[A] if (ivalence < 0) {
[A] if (valence > 2) {
[A] v.P = (OsdReadVertex(boundaryEdgeNeighbors[0]) +
[A] OsdReadVertex(boundaryEdgeNeighbors[1]) +
[A] 4.0f * pos)/6.0f;
[A] v.P = pos;
[A] v.e0 = (OsdReadVertex(boundaryEdgeNeighbors[0]) -
[A] OsdReadVertex(boundaryEdgeNeighbors[1]))/6.0;
[A] float k = float(float(valence) - 1.0f);    //k is the number of faces
[A] float c = cos(M_PI/k);
[A] float s = sin(M_PI/k);
[A] float gamma = -(4.0f*s)/(3.0f*k+c);
[A] float alpha_0k = -((1.0f+2.0f*c)*sqrt(1.0f+c))/((3.0f*k+c)*sqrt(1.0f-c));
[A] float beta_0 = s/(3.0f*k + c);
[A] int idx_diagonal = OsdReadVertexIndex(vID, 2*zerothNeighbor + 1);
[A] v.e1 = gamma * pos +
[A] alpha_0k * OsdReadVertex(boundaryEdgeNeighbors[0]) +
[A] alpha_0k * OsdReadVertex(boundaryEdgeNeighbors[1]) +
[A] beta_0 * diagonal;
[A] for (int x=1; x<valence - 1; ++x) {
[A] int curri = ((x + zerothNeighbor)%valence);
[A] float alpha = (4.0f*sin((M_PI * float(x))/k))/(3.0f*k+c);
[A] float beta = (sin((M_PI * float(x))/k) + sin((M_PI * float(x+1))/k))/(3.0f*k+c);
[A] int idx_neighbor = OsdReadVertexIndex(vID, 2*curri);
[A] idx_diagonal = OsdReadVertexIndex(vID, 2*curri + 1);
[A] diagonal = OsdReadVertex(idx_diagonal);
[A] v.e1 += alpha * neighbor + beta * diagonal;
[A] v.e1 /= 3.0f;
[A] OsdComputePerPatchVertexGregory(ivec3 patchParam, int ID, int primitiveID,
[A] in OsdPerVertexGregory v[4],
[A] out OsdPerPatchVertexGregory result)
[A] result.P = v[ID].P;
[A] int i = ID;
[A] int ip = (i+1)%4;
[A] int im = (i+3)%4;
[A] int valence = abs(v[i].valence);
[A] int n = valence;
[A] int start = OsdReadQuadOffset(primitiveID, i) & 0xff;
[A] int prev = (OsdReadQuadOffset(primitiveID, i) >> 8) & 0xff;
[A] int start_m = OsdReadQuadOffset(primitiveID, im) & 0xff;
[A] int prev_p = (OsdReadQuadOffset(primitiveID, ip) >> 8) & 0xff;
[A] int np = abs(v[ip].valence);
[A] int nm = abs(v[im].valence);
[A] // Control Vertices based on :
[A] // "Approximating Subdivision Surfaces with Gregory Patches
[A] //  for Hardware Tessellation"
[A] // Loop, Schaefer, Ni, Castano (ACM ToG Siggraph Asia 2009)
[A] //  P3         e3-      e2+         P2
[A] //     O--------O--------O--------O
[A] //     |        |        |        |
[A] //     |        | f3-    | f2+    |
[A] //     |        O        O        |
[A] // e3+ O------O            O------O e2-
[A] //     |     f3+          f2-     |
[A] //     |                          |
[A] //     |      f0-         f1+     |
[A] // e0- O------O            O------O e1+
[A] //     |        | f0+    | f1-    |
[A] //  P0         e0+      e1-         P1
[A] vec3 Em_ip;
[A] if (v[ip].valence < -2) {
[A] int j = (np + prev_p - v[ip].zerothNeighbor) % np;
[A] Em_ip = v[ip].P + cos((M_PI*j)/float(np-1))*v[ip].e0 + sin((M_PI*j)/float(np-1))*v[ip].e1;
[A] Em_ip = v[ip].P + v[ip].e0*cosfn(np, prev_p ) + v[ip].e1*sinfn(np, prev_p);
[A] vec3 Ep_im;
[A] if (v[im].valence < -2) {
[A] int j = (nm + start_m - v[im].zerothNeighbor) % nm;
[A] Ep_im = v[im].P + cos((M_PI*j)/float(nm-1))*v[im].e0 + sin((M_PI*j)/float(nm-1))*v[im].e1;
[A] Ep_im = v[im].P + v[im].e0*cosfn(nm, start_m) + v[im].e1*sinfn(nm, start_m);
[A] if (v[i].valence < 0) {
[A] n = (n-1)*2;
[A] if (v[im].valence < 0) {
[A] nm = (nm-1)*2;
[A] if (v[ip].valence < 0) {
[A] np = (np-1)*2;
[A] if (v[i].valence > 2) {
[A] result.Ep = v[i].P + v[i].e0*cosfn(n, start) + v[i].e1*sinfn(n, start);
[A] result.Em = v[i].P + v[i].e0*cosfn(n, prev ) + v[i].e1*sinfn(n, prev);
[A] float s1=3-2*cosfn(n,1)-cosfn(np,1);
[A] float s2=2*cosfn(n,1);
[A] result.Fp = (cosfn(np,1)*v[i].P + s1*result.Ep + s2*Em_ip + v[i].r[start])/3.0f;
[A] s1 = 3.0f-2.0f*cos(2.0f*M_PI/float(n))-cos(2.0f*M_PI/float(nm));
[A] result.Fm = (cosfn(nm,1)*v[i].P + s1*result.Em + s2*Ep_im - v[i].r[prev])/3.0f;
[A] } else if (v[i].valence < -2) {
[A] int j = (valence + start - v[i].zerothNeighbor) % valence;
[A] result.Ep = v[i].P + cos((M_PI*j)/float(valence-1))*v[i].e0 + sin((M_PI*j)/float(valence-1))*v[i].e1;
[A] j = (valence + prev - v[i].zerothNeighbor) % valence;
[A] result.Em = v[i].P + cos((M_PI*j)/float(valence-1))*v[i].e0 + sin((M_PI*j)/float(valence-1))*v[i].e1;
[A] vec3 Rp = ((-2.0f * v[i].org - 1.0f * v[im].org) + (2.0f * v[ip].org + 1.0f * v[(i+2)%4].org))/3.0f;
[A] vec3 Rm = ((-2.0f * v[i].org - 1.0f * v[ip].org) + (2.0f * v[im].org + 1.0f * v[(i+2)%4].org))/3.0f;
[A] float s1 = 3-2*cosfn(n,1)-cosfn(np,1);
[A] float s2 = 2*cosfn(n,1);
[A] s1 = 3-2*cosfn(n,1)-cosfn(np,1);
[A] result.Fp = result.Fm = (cosfn(np,1)*v[i].P + s1*result.Ep + s2*Em_ip + v[i].r[start])/3.0f;
[A] } else if (v[ip].valence < 0) {
[A] s1 = 3.0f-2.0f*cos(2.0f*M_PI/n)-cos(2.0f*M_PI/nm);
[A] result.Fm = result.Fp = (cosfn(nm,1)*v[i].P + s1*result.Em + s2*Ep_im - v[i].r[prev])/3.0f;
[A] } else if (v[i].valence == -2) {
[A] result.Ep = (2.0f * v[i].org + v[ip].org)/3.0f;
[A] result.Em = (2.0f * v[i].org + v[im].org)/3.0f;
[A] result.Fp = result.Fm = (4.0f * v[i].org + v[(i+2)%n].org + 2.0f * v[ip].org + 2.0f * v[im].org)/9.0f;
[A] #else // not OSD_PATCH_GREGORY_BOUNDARY
[A] result.Ep = v[i].P + v[i].e0 * cosfn(n, start) + v[i].e1*sinfn(n, start);
[A] result.Em = v[i].P + v[i].e0 * cosfn(n, prev ) + v[i].e1*sinfn(n, prev);
[A] vec3 Em_ip = v[ip].P + v[ip].e0 * cosfn(np, prev_p ) + v[ip].e1*sinfn(np, prev_p);
[A] vec3 Ep_im = v[im].P + v[im].e0 * cosfn(nm, start_m) + v[im].e1*sinfn(nm, start_m);
[A] #endif  // OSD_PATCH_GREGORY || OSD_PATCH_GREGORY_BOUNDARY
[A] // Legacy Face-varying
[A] uniform samplerBuffer OsdFVarDataBuffer;
[A] #ifndef OSD_FVAR_WIDTH
[A] #define OSD_FVAR_WIDTH 0
[A] // ------ extract from quads (catmark, bilinear) ---------
[A] // XXX: only linear interpolation is supported
[A] #define OSD_COMPUTE_FACE_VARYING_1(result, fvarOffset, tessCoord)           {                                                                           float v[4];                                                             int primOffset = OsdGetPatchIndex(gl_PrimitiveID) * 4;                  for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {                                               int index = (primOffset+i)*OSD_FVAR_WIDTH + fvarOffset;                 v[i] = texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index).s                       }                                                                       result = mix(mix(v[0], v[1], tessCoord.s),                                           mix(v[3], v[2], tessCoord.s),                                           tessCoord.t);                                          }
[A] #define OSD_COMPUTE_FACE_VARYING_2(result, fvarOffset, tessCoord)           {                                                                           vec2 v[4];                                                              int primOffset = OsdGetPatchIndex(gl_PrimitiveID) * 4;                  for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {                                               int index = (primOffset+i)*OSD_FVAR_WIDTH + fvarOffset;                 v[i] = vec2(texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index).s,                                 texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 1).s);            }                                                                       result = mix(mix(v[0], v[1], tessCoord.s),                                           mix(v[3], v[2], tessCoord.s),                                           tessCoord.t);                                          }
[A] #define OSD_COMPUTE_FACE_VARYING_3(result, fvarOffset, tessCoord)           {                                                                           vec3 v[4];                                                              int primOffset = OsdGetPatchIndex(gl_PrimitiveID) * 4;                  for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {                                               int index = (primOffset+i)*OSD_FVAR_WIDTH + fvarOffset;                 v[i] = vec3(texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index).s,                                 texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 1).s,                             texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 2).s);            }                                                                       result = mix(mix(v[0], v[1], tessCoord.s),                                           mix(v[3], v[2], tessCoord.s),                                           tessCoord.t);                                          }
[A] #define OSD_COMPUTE_FACE_VARYING_4(result, fvarOffset, tessCoord)           {                                                                           vec4 v[4];                                                              int primOffset = OsdGetPatchIndex(gl_PrimitiveID) * 4;                  for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {                                               int index = (primOffset+i)*OSD_FVAR_WIDTH + fvarOffset;                 v[i] = vec4(texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index).s,                                 texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 1).s,                             texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 2).s,                             texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 3).s);            }                                                                       result = mix(mix(v[0], v[1], tessCoord.s),                                           mix(v[3], v[2], tessCoord.s),                                           tessCoord.t);                                          }
[A] // ------ extract from triangles barycentric (loop) ---------
[A] // XXX: no interpolation supported
[A] #define OSD_COMPUTE_FACE_VARYING_TRI_1(result, fvarOffset, triVert)         {                                                                           int primOffset = OsdGetPatchIndex(gl_PrimitiveID) * 3;                  int index = (primOffset+triVert)*OSD_FVAR_WIDTH + fvarOffset;           result = texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index).s;                    }
[A] #define OSD_COMPUTE_FACE_VARYING_TRI_2(result, fvarOffset, triVert)         {                                                                           int primOffset = OsdGetPatchIndex(gl_PrimitiveID) * 3;                  int index = (primOffset+triVert)*OSD_FVAR_WIDTH + fvarOffset;           result = vec2(texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index).s,                                 texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 1).s);          }
[A] #define OSD_COMPUTE_FACE_VARYING_TRI_3(result, fvarOffset, triVert)         {                                                                           int primOffset = OsdGetPatchIndex(gl_PrimitiveID) * 3;                  int index = (primOffset+triVert)*OSD_FVAR_WIDTH + fvarOffset;           result = vec3(texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index).s,                                 texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 1).s,                             texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 2).s);          }
[A] #define OSD_COMPUTE_FACE_VARYING_TRI_4(result, fvarOffset, triVert)         {                                                                           int primOffset = OsdGetPatchIndex(gl_PrimitiveID) * 3;                  int index = (primOffset+triVert)*OSD_FVAR_WIDTH + fvarOffset;           result = vec4(texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index).s,                                 texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 1).s,                             texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 2).s,                             texelFetch(OsdFVarDataBuffer, index + 3).s);          }
