[A] // OpenCL binding uses typedef to provide the required "struct" type specifier.
[A] typedef struct OsdPatchParam OsdPatchParam;
[A] typedef struct OsdPatchArray OsdPatchArray;
[A] typedef struct OsdPatchCoord OsdPatchCoord;
[A] // Osd reflection of Far::PatchDescriptor
[A] #define OSD_PATCH_DESCRIPTOR_QUADS            3
[A] #define OSD_PATCH_DESCRIPTOR_TRIANGLES        4
[A] #define OSD_PATCH_DESCRIPTOR_LOOP             5
[A] #define OSD_PATCH_DESCRIPTOR_REGULAR          6
[A] #define OSD_PATCH_DESCRIPTOR_GREGORY_BASIS    9
[A] #define OSD_PATCH_DESCRIPTOR_GREGORY_TRIANGLE 10
[A] // Osd reflection of Osd::PatchCoord
[A] struct OsdPatchCoord {
[A] int arrayIndex;
[A] int patchIndex;
[A] int vertIndex;
[A] float s;
[A] float t;
[A] OSD_FUNCTION_STORAGE_CLASS
[A] OsdPatchCoord
[A] OsdPatchCoordInit(
[A] int arrayIndex, int patchIndex, int vertIndex, float s, float t)
[A] OsdPatchCoord coord;
[A] coord.arrayIndex = arrayIndex;
[A] coord.patchIndex = patchIndex;
[A] coord.vertIndex = vertIndex;
[A] coord.s = s;
[A] coord.t = t;
[A] return coord;
[A] // Osd reflection of Osd::PatchArray
[A] struct OsdPatchArray {
[A] int regDesc;
[A] int desc;
[A] int numPatches;
[A] int indexBase;
[A] int stride;
[A] int primitiveIdBase;
[A] OsdPatchArray
[A] OsdPatchArrayInit(
[A] int regDesc, int desc,
[A] int numPatches, int indexBase, int stride, int primitiveIdBase)
[A] OsdPatchArray array;
[A] array.regDesc = regDesc;
[A] array.desc = desc;
[A] array.numPatches = numPatches;
[A] array.indexBase = indexBase;
[A] array.stride = stride;
[A] array.primitiveIdBase = primitiveIdBase;
[A] return array;
[A] // Osd reflection of Osd::PatchParam
[A] struct OsdPatchParam {
[A] int field0;
[A] int field1;
[A] float sharpness;
[A] OsdPatchParam
[A] OsdPatchParamInit(int field0, int field1, float sharpness)
[A] OsdPatchParam param;
[A] param.field0 = field0;
[A] param.field1 = field1;
[A] param.sharpness = sharpness;
[A] return param;
[A] OsdPatchParamGetFaceId(OsdPatchParam param)
[A] return (param.field0 & 0xfffffff);
[A] OsdPatchParamGetU(OsdPatchParam param)
[A] return ((param.field1 >> 22) & 0x3ff);
[A] OsdPatchParamGetV(OsdPatchParam param)
[A] return ((param.field1 >> 12) & 0x3ff);
[A] OsdPatchParamGetTransition(OsdPatchParam param)
[A] return ((param.field0 >> 28) & 0xf);
[A] OsdPatchParamGetBoundary(OsdPatchParam param)
[A] return ((param.field1 >> 7) & 0x1f);
[A] OsdPatchParamGetNonQuadRoot(OsdPatchParam param)
[A] return ((param.field1 >> 4) & 0x1);
[A] OsdPatchParamGetDepth(OsdPatchParam param)
[A] return (param.field1 & 0xf);
[A] OSD_REAL
[A] OsdPatchParamGetParamFraction(OsdPatchParam param)
[A] return 1.0f / OSD_REAL_CAST(1 <<
[A] (OsdPatchParamGetDepth(param) - OsdPatchParamGetNonQuadRoot(param)));
[A] OsdPatchParamIsRegular(OsdPatchParam param)
[A] return (((param.field1 >> 5) & 0x1) != 0);
[A] OsdPatchParamIsTriangleRotated(OsdPatchParam param)
[A] return ((OsdPatchParamGetU(param) + OsdPatchParamGetV(param)) >=
[A] (1 << OsdPatchParamGetDepth(param)));
[A] OsdPatchParamNormalize(
[A] OsdPatchParam param,
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, uv, 2))
[A] OSD_REAL fracInv = 1.0f / OsdPatchParamGetParamFraction(param);
[A] uv[0] = uv[0] * fracInv - OSD_REAL_CAST(OsdPatchParamGetU(param));
[A] uv[1] = uv[1] * fracInv - OSD_REAL_CAST(OsdPatchParamGetV(param));
[A] OsdPatchParamUnnormalize(
[A] OSD_REAL frac = OsdPatchParamGetParamFraction(param);
[A] uv[0] = (uv[0] + OSD_REAL_CAST(OsdPatchParamGetU(param))) * frac;
[A] uv[1] = (uv[1] + OSD_REAL_CAST(OsdPatchParamGetV(param))) * frac;
[A] OsdPatchParamNormalizeTriangle(
[A] if (OsdPatchParamIsTriangleRotated(param)) {
[A] int depthFactor = 1 << OsdPatchParamGetDepth(param);
[A] uv[0] = OSD_REAL_CAST(depthFactor - OsdPatchParamGetU(param)) - (uv[0] * fracInv);
[A] uv[1] = OSD_REAL_CAST(depthFactor - OsdPatchParamGetV(param)) - (uv[1] * fracInv);
[A] OsdPatchParamUnnormalizeTriangle(
[A] uv[0] = (OSD_REAL_CAST(depthFactor - OsdPatchParamGetU(param)) - uv[0]) * frac;
[A] uv[1] = (OSD_REAL_CAST(depthFactor - OsdPatchParamGetV(param)) - uv[1]) * frac;
[A] OsdPatchParamUnnormalize(param, uv);
[A] #endif /* OPENSUBDIV3_OSD_PATCH_BASIS_COMMON_TYPES_H */
[A] //   Copyright 2016-2018 Pixar
[A] #ifndef OPENSUBDIV3_OSD_PATCH_BASIS_COMMON_H
[A] #define OPENSUBDIV3_OSD_PATCH_BASIS_COMMON_H
[A] // template <typename REAL>
[A] Osd_EvalBasisLinear(OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wP, 4),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDs, 4),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDt, 4),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDss, 4),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDst, 4),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDtt, 4)) {
[A] OSD_REAL sC = 1.0f - s;
[A] OSD_REAL tC = 1.0f - t;
[A] if (OSD_OPTIONAL(wP)) {
[A] wP[0] = sC * tC;
[A] wP[1] =  s * tC;
[A] wP[2] =  s * t;
[A] wP[3] = sC * t;
[A] if (OSD_OPTIONAL(wDs && wDt)) {
[A] wDs[0] = -tC;
[A] wDs[1] =  tC;
[A] wDs[2] =   t;
[A] wDs[3] =  -t;
[A] wDt[0] = -sC;
[A] wDt[1] =  -s;
[A] wDt[2] =   s;
[A] wDt[3] =  sC;
[A] if (OSD_OPTIONAL(wDss && wDst && wDtt)) {
[A] for(int i=0;i<4;i++) {
[A] wDss[i] = 0.0f;
[A] wDtt[i] = 0.0f;
[A] wDst[0] =  1.0f;
[A] wDst[1] = -1.0f;
[A] wDst[2] =  1.0f;
[A] wDst[3] = -1.0f;
[A] return 4;
[A] // namespace {
[A] //  Cubic BSpline curve basis evaluation:
[A] Osd_evalBSplineCurve(OSD_REAL t,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDP, 4),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDP2, 4)) {
[A] const OSD_REAL one6th = OSD_REAL_CAST(1.0f / 6.0f);
[A] OSD_REAL t2 = t * t;
[A] OSD_REAL t3 = t * t2;
[A] wP[0] = one6th * (1.0f - 3.0f*(t -      t2) -      t3);
[A] wP[1] = one6th * (4.0f           - 6.0f*t2  + 3.0f*t3);
[A] wP[2] = one6th * (1.0f + 3.0f*(t +      t2  -      t3));
[A] wP[3] = one6th * (                                 t3);
[A] if (OSD_OPTIONAL(wDP)) {
[A] wDP[0] = -0.5f*t2 +      t - 0.5f;
[A] wDP[1] =  1.5f*t2 - 2.0f*t;
[A] wDP[2] = -1.5f*t2 +      t + 0.5f;
[A] wDP[3] =  0.5f*t2;
[A] if (OSD_OPTIONAL(wDP2)) {
[A] wDP2[0] = -       t + 1.0f;
[A] wDP2[1] =  3.0f * t - 2.0f;
[A] wDP2[2] = -3.0f * t + 1.0f;
[A] wDP2[3] =         t;
[A] //  Weight adjustments to account for phantom end points:
[A] Osd_adjustBSplineBoundaryWeights(
[A] int boundary,
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, w, 16)) {
[A] if ((boundary & 1) != 0) {
[A] w[i + 8] -= w[i + 0];
[A] w[i + 4] += w[i + 0] * 2.0f;
[A] w[i + 0]  = 0.0f;
[A] if ((boundary & 2) != 0) {
[A] for (int i = 0; i < 16; i += 4) {
[A] w[i + 1] -= w[i + 3];
[A] w[i + 2] += w[i + 3] * 2.0f;
[A] w[i + 3]  = 0.0f;
[A] if ((boundary & 4) != 0) {
[A] w[i +  4] -= w[i + 12];
[A] w[i +  8] += w[i + 12] * 2.0f;
[A] w[i + 12]  = 0.0f;
[A] if ((boundary & 8) != 0) {
[A] w[i + 2] -= w[i + 0];
[A] w[i + 1] += w[i + 0] * 2.0f;
[A] Osd_boundBasisBSpline(
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wP, 16),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDs, 16),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDt, 16),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDss, 16),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDst, 16),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDtt, 16)) {
[A] Osd_adjustBSplineBoundaryWeights(boundary, wP);
[A] Osd_adjustBSplineBoundaryWeights(boundary, wDs);
[A] Osd_adjustBSplineBoundaryWeights(boundary, wDt);
[A] Osd_adjustBSplineBoundaryWeights(boundary, wDss);
[A] Osd_adjustBSplineBoundaryWeights(boundary, wDst);
[A] Osd_adjustBSplineBoundaryWeights(boundary, wDtt);
[A] // } // end namespace
[A] Osd_EvalBasisBSpline(OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wP, 16),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDs, 16),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDt, 16),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDss, 16),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDst, 16),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDtt, 16)) {
[A] OSD_REAL sWeights[4], tWeights[4], dsWeights[4], dtWeights[4], dssWeights[4], dttWeights[4];
[A] Osd_evalBSplineCurve(s, sWeights, OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDs, dsWeights), OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDss, dssWeights));
[A] Osd_evalBSplineCurve(t, tWeights, OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDt, dtWeights), OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDtt, dttWeights));
[A] for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
[A] wP[4*i+j] = sWeights[j] * tWeights[i];
[A] wDs[4*i+j] = dsWeights[j] * tWeights[i];
[A] wDt[4*i+j] = sWeights[j] * dtWeights[i];
[A] wDss[4*i+j] = dssWeights[j] * tWeights[i];
[A] wDst[4*i+j] = dsWeights[j] * dtWeights[i];
[A] wDtt[4*i+j] = sWeights[j] * dttWeights[i];
[A] return 16;
[A] //  Cubic Bezier curve basis evaluation:
[A] Osd_evalBezierCurve(
[A] OSD_REAL t,
[A] // The four uniform cubic Bezier basis functions (in terms of t and its
[A] // complement tC) evaluated at t:
[A] OSD_REAL t2 = t*t;
[A] OSD_REAL tC2 = tC * tC;
[A] wP[0] = tC2 * tC;
[A] wP[1] = tC2 * t * 3.0f;
[A] wP[2] = t2 * tC * 3.0f;
[A] wP[3] = t2 * t;
[A] // Derivatives of the above four basis functions at t:
[A] wDP[0] = -3.0f * tC2;
[A] wDP[1] =  9.0f * t2 - 12.0f * t + 3.0f;
[A] wDP[2] = -9.0f * t2 +  6.0f * t;
[A] wDP[3] =  3.0f * t2;
[A] // Second derivatives of the basis functions at t:
[A] wDP2[0] =   6.0f * tC;
[A] wDP2[1] =  18.0f * t - 12.0f;
[A] wDP2[2] = -18.0f * t +  6.0f;
[A] wDP2[3] =   6.0f * t;
[A] Osd_EvalBasisBezier(OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t,
[A] Osd_evalBezierCurve(s, OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wP, sWeights), OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDs, dsWeights), OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDss, dssWeights));
[A] Osd_evalBezierCurve(t, OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wP, tWeights), OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDt, dtWeights), OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDtt, dttWeights));
[A] Osd_EvalBasisGregory(OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wP, 20),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDs, 20),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDt, 20),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDss, 20),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDst, 20),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDtt, 20)) {
[A] //  Indices of boundary and interior points and their corresponding Bezier points
[A] //  (this can be reduced with more direct indexing and unrolling of loops):
[A] OSD_DATA_STORAGE_CLASS const int boundaryGregory[12] = OSD_ARRAY_12(int, 0, 1, 7, 5, 2, 6, 16, 12, 15, 17, 11, 10 );
[A] OSD_DATA_STORAGE_CLASS const int boundaryBezSCol[12] = OSD_ARRAY_12(int, 0, 1, 2, 3, 0, 3,  0,  3,  0,  1,  2,  3 );
[A] OSD_DATA_STORAGE_CLASS const int boundaryBezTRow[12] = OSD_ARRAY_12(int, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1,  2,  2,  3,  3,  3,  3 );
[A] OSD_DATA_STORAGE_CLASS const int interiorGregory[8] = OSD_ARRAY_8(int, 3, 4,  8, 9,  13, 14,  18, 19 );
[A] OSD_DATA_STORAGE_CLASS const int interiorBezSCol[8] = OSD_ARRAY_8(int, 1, 1,  2, 2,   2,  2,   1,  1 );
[A] OSD_DATA_STORAGE_CLASS const int interiorBezTRow[8] = OSD_ARRAY_8(int, 1, 1,  1, 1,   2,  2,   2,  2 );
[A] //  Bezier basis functions are denoted with B while the rational multipliers for the
[A] //  interior points will be denoted G -- so we have B(s), B(t) and G(s,t):
[A] //  Directional Bezier basis functions B at s and t:
[A] OSD_REAL Bs[4], Bds[4], Bdss[4];
[A] OSD_REAL Bt[4], Bdt[4], Bdtt[4];
[A] Osd_evalBezierCurve(s, Bs, OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDs, Bds), OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDss, Bdss));
[A] Osd_evalBezierCurve(t, Bt, OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDt, Bdt), OSD_OPTIONAL_INIT(wDtt, Bdtt));
[A] //  Rational multipliers G at s and t:
[A] //  Use <= here to avoid compiler warnings -- the sums should always be non-negative:
[A] OSD_REAL df0 = s  + t;   df0 = (df0 <= 0.0f) ? 1.0f : (1.0f / df0);
[A] OSD_REAL df1 = sC + t;   df1 = (df1 <= 0.0f) ? 1.0f : (1.0f / df1);
[A] OSD_REAL df2 = sC + tC;  df2 = (df2 <= 0.0f) ? 1.0f : (1.0f / df2);
[A] OSD_REAL df3 = s  + tC;  df3 = (df3 <= 0.0f) ? 1.0f : (1.0f / df3);
[A] //  Make sure the G[i] for pairs of interior points sum to 1 in all cases:
[A] OSD_REAL G[8] = OSD_ARRAY_8(OSD_REAL,  s*df0, (1.0f -  s*df0),
[A] t*df1, (1.0f -  t*df1),
[A] sC*df2, (1.0f - sC*df2),
[A] tC*df3, (1.0f - tC*df3) );
[A] //  Combined weights for boundary and interior points:
[A] wP[boundaryGregory[i]] = Bs[boundaryBezSCol[i]] * Bt[boundaryBezTRow[i]];
[A] for (int j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
[A] wP[interiorGregory[j]] = Bs[interiorBezSCol[j]] * Bt[interiorBezTRow[j]] * G[j];
[A] //  For derivatives, the basis functions for the interior points are rational and ideally
[A] //  require appropriate differentiation, i.e. product rule for the combination of B and G
[A] //  and the quotient rule for the rational G itself.  As initially proposed by Loop et al
[A] //  though, the approximation using the 16 Bezier points arising from the G(s,t) has
[A] //  proved adequate (and is what the GPU shaders use) so we continue to use that here.
[A] //  An implementation of the true derivatives is provided and conditionally compiled for
[A] //  those that require it, e.g.:
[A] //    dclyde's note: skipping half of the product rule like this does seem to change the
[A] //    result a lot in my tests.  This is not a runtime bottleneck for cloth sims anyway
[A] //    so I'm just using the accurate version.
[A] bool find_second_partials = OSD_OPTIONAL(wDs && wDst && wDtt);
[A] //  Combined weights for boundary points -- simple tensor products:
[A] int iDst = boundaryGregory[i];
[A] int tRow = boundaryBezTRow[i];
[A] int sCol = boundaryBezSCol[i];
[A] wDs[iDst] = Bds[sCol] * Bt[tRow];
[A] wDt[iDst] = Bdt[tRow] * Bs[sCol];
[A] if (find_second_partials) {
[A] wDss[iDst] = Bdss[sCol] * Bt[tRow];
[A] wDst[iDst] = Bds[sCol] * Bdt[tRow];
[A] wDtt[iDst] = Bs[sCol] * Bdtt[tRow];
[A] #ifndef OPENSUBDIV_GREGORY_EVAL_TRUE_DERIVATIVES
[A] //  Approximation to the true Gregory derivatives by differentiating the Bezier patch
[A] //  unique to the given (s,t), i.e. having F = (g^+ * f^+) + (g^- * f^-) as its four
[A] //  interior points:
[A] //  Combined weights for interior points -- tensor products with G+ or G-:
[A] int iDst = interiorGregory[j];
[A] int tRow = interiorBezTRow[j];
[A] int sCol = interiorBezSCol[j];
[A] wDs[iDst] = Bds[sCol] * Bt[tRow] * G[j];
[A] wDt[iDst] = Bdt[tRow] * Bs[sCol] * G[j];
[A] wDss[iDst] = Bdss[sCol] * Bt[tRow] * G[j];
[A] wDst[iDst] = Bds[sCol] * Bdt[tRow] * G[j];
[A] wDtt[iDst] = Bs[sCol] * Bdtt[tRow] * G[j];
[A] //  True Gregory derivatives using appropriate differentiation of composite functions:
[A] //  Note that for G(s,t) = N(s,t) / D(s,t), all N' and D' are trivial constants (which
[A] //  simplifies things for higher order derivatives).  And while each pair of functions
[A] //  G (i.e. the G+ and G- corresponding to points f+ and f-) must sum to 1 to ensure
[A] //  Bezier equivalence (when f+ = f-), the pairs of G' must similarly sum to 0.  So we
[A] //  can potentially compute only one of the pair and negate the result for the other
[A] //  (and with 4 or 8 computations involving these constants, this is all very SIMD
[A] //  friendly...) but for now we treat all 8 independently for simplicity.
[A] //float N[8] = OSD_ARRAY_8(float,    s,     t,      t,     sC,      sC,     tC,      tC,     s );
[A] OSD_REAL D[8] = OSD_ARRAY_8(OSD_REAL,  df0,   df0,    df1,    df1,     df2,    df2,     df3,   df3 );
[A] OSD_DATA_STORAGE_CLASS const OSD_REAL Nds[8] = OSD_ARRAY_8(OSD_REAL, 1.0f, 0.0f,  0.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f,  0.0f,  0.0f,  1.0f );
[A] OSD_DATA_STORAGE_CLASS const OSD_REAL Ndt[8] = OSD_ARRAY_8(OSD_REAL, 0.0f, 1.0f,  1.0f,  0.0f,  0.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f,  0.0f );
[A] OSD_DATA_STORAGE_CLASS const OSD_REAL Dds[8] = OSD_ARRAY_8(OSD_REAL, 1.0f, 1.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f,  1.0f,  1.0f );
[A] OSD_DATA_STORAGE_CLASS const OSD_REAL Ddt[8] = OSD_ARRAY_8(OSD_REAL, 1.0f, 1.0f,  1.0f,  1.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f );
[A] //  Combined weights for interior points -- (scaled) combinations of B, B', G and G':
[A] for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) {
[A] int iDst = interiorGregory[k];
[A] int tRow = interiorBezTRow[k];
[A] int sCol = interiorBezSCol[k];
[A] //  Quotient rule for G' (re-expressed in terms of G to simplify (and D = 1/D)):
[A] OSD_REAL Gds = (Nds[k] - Dds[k] * G[k]) * D[k];
[A] OSD_REAL Gdt = (Ndt[k] - Ddt[k] * G[k]) * D[k];
[A] //  Product rule combining B and B' with G and G':
[A] wDs[iDst] = (Bds[sCol] * G[k] + Bs[sCol] * Gds) * Bt[tRow];
[A] wDt[iDst] = (Bdt[tRow] * G[k] + Bt[tRow] * Gdt) * Bs[sCol];
[A] OSD_REAL Dsqr_inv = D[k]*D[k];
[A] OSD_REAL Gdss = 2.0f * Dds[k] * Dsqr_inv * (G[k] * Dds[k] - Nds[k]);
[A] OSD_REAL Gdst = Dsqr_inv * (2.0f * G[k] * Dds[k] * Ddt[k] - Nds[k] * Ddt[k] - Ndt[k] * Dds[k]);
[A] OSD_REAL Gdtt = 2.0f * Ddt[k] * Dsqr_inv * (G[k] * Ddt[k] - Ndt[k]);
[A] wDss[iDst] = (Bdss[sCol] * G[k] + 2.0f * Bds[sCol] * Gds + Bs[sCol] * Gdss) * Bt[tRow];
[A] wDst[iDst] =  Bt[tRow] * (Bs[sCol] * Gdst + Bds[sCol] * Gdt) +
[A] Bdt[tRow] * (Bds[sCol] * G[k] + Bs[sCol] * Gds);
[A] wDtt[iDst] = (Bdtt[tRow] * G[k] + 2.0f * Bdt[tRow] * Gdt + Bt[tRow] * Gdtt) * Bs[sCol];
[A] return 20;
[A] Osd_EvalBasisLinearTri(OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wP, 3),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDs, 3),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDt, 3),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDss, 3),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDst, 3),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDtt, 3)) {
[A] wP[0] = 1.0f - s - t;
[A] wP[1] = s;
[A] wP[2] = t;
[A] wDs[0] = -1.0f;
[A] wDs[1] =  1.0f;
[A] wDs[2] =  0.0f;
[A] wDt[0] = -1.0f;
[A] wDt[1] =  0.0f;
[A] wDt[2] =  1.0f;
[A] wDss[0] = wDss[1] = wDss[2] = 0.0f;
[A] wDst[0] = wDst[1] = wDst[2] = 0.0f;
[A] wDtt[0] = wDtt[1] = wDtt[2] = 0.0f;
[A] return 3;
[A] Osd_evalBivariateMonomialsQuartic(
[A] OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, M, 15)) {
[A] M[0] = 1.0;
[A] M[1] = s;
[A] M[2] = t;
[A] M[3] = s * s;
[A] M[4] = s * t;
[A] M[5] = t * t;
[A] M[6] = M[3] * s;
[A] M[7] = M[4] * s;
[A] M[8] = M[4] * t;
[A] M[9] = M[5] * t;
[A] M[10] = M[6] * s;
[A] M[11] = M[7] * s;
[A] M[12] = M[3] * M[5];
[A] M[13] = M[8] * t;
[A] M[14] = M[9] * t;
[A] Osd_evalBoxSplineTriDerivWeights(
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, /*stMonomials*/M, 15),
[A] int ds, int dt,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, w, 12)) {
[A] // const OSD_REAL M[15] = stMonomials;
[A] OSD_REAL S = 1.0f;
[A] int totalOrder = ds + dt;
[A] if (totalOrder == 0) {
[A] S *= OSD_REAL_CAST(1.0 / 12.0);
[A] w[0]  = S * (1 - 2*M[1] - 4*M[2]          + 6*M[4] + 6*M[5] + 2*M[6]          - 6*M[8] - 4*M[9] -   M[10] - 2*M[11] + 2*M[13] +   M[14]);
[A] w[1]  = S * (1 + 2*M[1] - 2*M[2]          - 6*M[4]          - 4*M[6]          + 6*M[8] + 2*M[9] + 2*M[10] + 4*M[11] - 2*M[13] -   M[14]);
[A] w[2]  = S * (                                                 2*M[6]                            -   M[10] - 2*M[11]                    );
[A] w[3]  = S * (1 - 4*M[1] - 2*M[2] + 6*M[3] + 6*M[4]          - 4*M[6] - 6*M[7]          + 2*M[9] +   M[10] + 2*M[11] - 2*M[13] -   M[14]);
[A] w[4]  = S * (6                   -12*M[3] -12*M[4] -12*M[5] + 8*M[6] +12*M[7] +12*M[8] + 8*M[9] -   M[10] - 2*M[11] - 2*M[13] -   M[14]);
[A] w[5]  = S * (1 + 4*M[1] + 2*M[2] + 6*M[3] + 6*M[4]          - 4*M[6] - 6*M[7] -12*M[8] - 4*M[9] -   M[10] - 2*M[11] + 4*M[13] + 2*M[14]);
[A] w[6]  = S * (                                                                                       M[10] + 2*M[11]                    );
[A] w[7]  = S * (1 - 2*M[1] + 2*M[2]          - 6*M[4]          + 2*M[6] + 6*M[7]          - 4*M[9] -   M[10] - 2*M[11] + 4*M[13] + 2*M[14]);
[A] w[8]  = S * (1 + 2*M[1] + 4*M[2]          + 6*M[4] + 6*M[5] - 4*M[6] -12*M[7] - 6*M[8] - 4*M[9] + 2*M[10] + 4*M[11] - 2*M[13] -   M[14]);
[A] w[9]  = S * (                                                 2*M[6] + 6*M[7] + 6*M[8] + 2*M[9] -   M[10] - 2*M[11] - 2*M[13] -   M[14]);
[A] w[10] = S * (                                                                            2*M[9]                     - 2*M[13] -   M[14]);
[A] w[11] = S * (                                                                                                         2*M[13] +   M[14]);
[A] } else if (totalOrder == 1) {
[A] S *= OSD_REAL_CAST(1.0 / 6.0);
[A] if (ds != 0) {
[A] w[0]  = S * (-1          + 3*M[2] + 3*M[3]          - 3*M[5] - 2*M[6] - 3*M[7] +   M[9]);
[A] w[1]  = S * ( 1          - 3*M[2] - 6*M[3]          + 3*M[5] + 4*M[6] + 6*M[7] -   M[9]);
[A] w[2]  = S * (                       3*M[3]                   - 2*M[6] - 3*M[7]         );
[A] w[3]  = S * (-2 + 6*M[1] + 3*M[2] - 6*M[3] - 6*M[4]          + 2*M[6] + 3*M[7] -   M[9]);
[A] w[4]  = S * (   -12*M[1] - 6*M[2] +12*M[3] +12*M[4] + 6*M[5] - 2*M[6] - 3*M[7] -   M[9]);
[A] w[5]  = S * ( 2 + 6*M[1] + 3*M[2] - 6*M[3] - 6*M[4] - 6*M[5] - 2*M[6] - 3*M[7] + 2*M[9]);
[A] w[6]  = S * (                                                  2*M[6] + 3*M[7]         );
[A] w[7]  = S * (-1          - 3*M[2] + 3*M[3] + 6*M[4]          - 2*M[6] - 3*M[7] + 2*M[9]);
[A] w[8]  = S * ( 1          + 3*M[2] - 6*M[3] -12*M[4] - 3*M[5] + 4*M[6] + 6*M[7] -   M[9]);
[A] w[9]  = S * (                       3*M[3] + 6*M[4] + 3*M[5] - 2*M[6] - 3*M[7] -   M[9]);
[A] w[10] = S * (                                                                  -   M[9]);
[A] w[11] = S * (                                                                      M[9]);
[A] w[0]  = S * (-2 + 3*M[1] + 6*M[2]          - 6*M[4] - 6*M[5]  -   M[6] + 3*M[8] + 2*M[9]);
[A] w[1]  = S * (-1 - 3*M[1]                   + 6*M[4] + 3*M[5]  + 2*M[6] - 3*M[8] - 2*M[9]);
[A] w[2]  = S * (                                                 -   M[6]                  );
[A] w[3]  = S * (-1 + 3*M[1]          - 3*M[3]          + 3*M[5]  +   M[6] - 3*M[8] - 2*M[9]);
[A] w[4]  = S * (   - 6*M[1] -12*M[2] + 6*M[3] +12*M[4] +12*M[5]  -   M[6] - 3*M[8] - 2*M[9]);
[A] w[5]  = S * ( 1 + 3*M[1]          - 3*M[3] -12*M[4] - 6*M[5]  -   M[6] + 6*M[8] + 4*M[9]);
[A] w[6]  = S * (                                                 +   M[6]                  );
[A] w[7]  = S * ( 1 - 3*M[1]          + 3*M[3]          - 6*M[5]  -   M[6] + 6*M[8] + 4*M[9]);
[A] w[8]  = S * ( 2 + 3*M[1] + 6*M[2] - 6*M[3] - 6*M[4] - 6*M[5]  + 2*M[6] - 3*M[8] - 2*M[9]);
[A] w[9]  = S * (                     + 3*M[3] + 6*M[4] + 3*M[5]  -   M[6] - 3*M[8] - 2*M[9]);
[A] w[10] = S * (                                         3*M[5]           - 3*M[8] - 2*M[9]);
[A] w[11] = S * (                                                            3*M[8] + 2*M[9]);
[A] } else if (totalOrder == 2) {
[A] if (ds == 2) {
[A] w[0]  = S * (   +   M[1]          -   M[3] -   M[4]);
[A] w[1]  = S * (   - 2*M[1]          + 2*M[3] + 2*M[4]);
[A] w[2]  = S * (       M[1]          -   M[3] -   M[4]);
[A] w[3]  = S * ( 1 - 2*M[1] -   M[2] +   M[3] +   M[4]);
[A] w[4]  = S * (-2 + 4*M[1] + 2*M[2] -   M[3] -   M[4]);
[A] w[5]  = S * ( 1 - 2*M[1] -   M[2] -   M[3] -   M[4]);
[A] w[6]  = S * (                         M[3] +   M[4]);
[A] w[7]  = S * (   +   M[1] +   M[2] -   M[3] -   M[4]);
[A] w[8]  = S * (   - 2*M[1] - 2*M[2] + 2*M[3] + 2*M[4]);
[A] w[9]  = S * (       M[1] +   M[2] -   M[3] -   M[4]);
[A] w[10] =     0;
[A] w[11] =     0;
[A] } else if (dt == 2) {
[A] w[0]  = S * ( 1 -   M[1] - 2*M[2] +   M[4] +   M[5]);
[A] w[1]  = S * (   +   M[1] +   M[2] -   M[4] -   M[5]);
[A] w[2]  =     0;
[A] w[3]  = S * (            +   M[2] -   M[4] -   M[5]);
[A] w[4]  = S * (-2 + 2*M[1] + 4*M[2] -   M[4] -   M[5]);
[A] w[5]  = S * (   - 2*M[1] - 2*M[2] + 2*M[4] + 2*M[5]);
[A] w[6]  =     0;
[A] w[7]  = S * (            - 2*M[2] + 2*M[4] + 2*M[5]);
[A] w[8]  = S * ( 1 -   M[1] - 2*M[2] -   M[4] -   M[5]);
[A] w[9]  = S * (   +   M[1] +   M[2] -   M[4] -   M[5]);
[A] w[10] = S * (                M[2] -   M[4] -   M[5]);
[A] w[11] = S * (                         M[4] +   M[5]);
[A] S *= OSD_REAL_CAST(1.0 / 2.0);
[A] w[0]  = S * ( 1          - 2*M[2] -   M[3] +   M[5]);
[A] w[1]  = S * (-1          + 2*M[2] + 2*M[3] -   M[5]);
[A] w[2]  = S * (                     -   M[3]         );
[A] w[3]  = S * ( 1 - 2*M[1]          +   M[3] -   M[5]);
[A] w[4]  = S * (-2 + 4*M[1] + 4*M[2] -   M[3] -   M[5]);
[A] w[5]  = S * ( 1 - 2*M[1] - 4*M[2] -   M[3] + 2*M[5]);
[A] w[6]  = S * (                     +   M[3]         );
[A] w[7]  = S * (-1 + 2*M[1]          -   M[3] + 2*M[5]);
[A] w[8]  = S * ( 1 - 4*M[1] - 2*M[2] + 2*M[3] -   M[5]);
[A] w[9]  = S * (   + 2*M[1] + 2*M[2] -   M[3] -   M[5]);
[A] w[10] = S * (                              -   M[5]);
[A] w[11] = S * (                                  M[5]);
[A] // assert(totalOrder <= 2);
[A] Osd_adjustBoxSplineTriBoundaryWeights(
[A] int boundaryMask,
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, weights, 12)) {
[A] //  Determine boundary edges and vertices from the lower 3 and upper
[A] //  2 bits of the 5-bit mask:
[A] //  Boundary vertices only:
[A] //  Adjust weights for the 4 boundary points and 3 interior points
[A] //  to account for the 3 phantom points adjacent to each
[A] //  boundary edge:
[A] OSD_REAL w0 = weights[0];
[A] //  P0 = B1 + (B1 - I1)
[A] weights[4] += w0;
[A] weights[8] -= w0;
[A] //  P0 = B1 + (B0 - I0)
[A] weights[3] += w0;
[A] weights[7] -= w0;
[A] //  P1 = B1 + (B2 - I1)
[A] OSD_REAL w1 = weights[1];
[A] weights[4] += w1;
[A] weights[5] += w1;
[A] weights[8] -= w1;
[A] OSD_REAL w2 = weights[2];
[A] //  P2 = B2 + (B2 - I1)
[A] weights[5] += w2;
[A] weights[8] -= w2;
[A] //  P2 = B2 + (B3 - I2)
[A] weights[6] += w2;
[A] weights[9] -= w2;
[A] //  Clear weights for the phantom points:
[A] weights[0] = weights[1] = weights[2] = 0.0f;
[A] OSD_REAL w0 = weights[6];
[A] weights[5] += w0;
[A] weights[4] -= w0;
[A] weights[2] += w0;
[A] weights[1] -= w0;
[A] OSD_REAL w1 = weights[9];
[A] weights[8] += w1;
[A] weights[4] -= w1;
[A] OSD_REAL w2 = weights[11];
[A] weights[8] += w2;
[A] weights[4] -= w2;
[A] weights[8]  += w2;
[A] weights[10] += w2;
[A] weights[7]  -= w2;
[A] weights[6] = weights[9] = weights[11] = 0.0f;
[A] OSD_REAL w0 = weights[10];
[A] weights[8] += w0;
[A] weights[5] -= w0;
[A] weights[8]  += w0;
[A] weights[11] += w0;
[A] weights[9]  -= w0;
[A] OSD_REAL w1 = weights[7];
[A] weights[5] -= w1;
[A] OSD_REAL w2 = weights[3];
[A] weights[4] += w2;
[A] weights[5] -= w2;
[A] weights[0] += w2;
[A] weights[1] -= w2;
[A] weights[10] = weights[7] = weights[3] = 0.0f;
[A] //  Adjust weights for the 3 boundary points and the 2 interior
[A] //  points to account for the 2 phantom points adjacent to
[A] //  each boundary vertex:
[A] OSD_REAL w0 = weights[3];
[A] weights[7] += w0;
[A] OSD_REAL w1 = weights[0];
[A] weights[1] += w1;
[A] weights[3] = weights[0] = 0.0f;
[A] OSD_REAL w0 = weights[2];
[A] weights[1] += w0;
[A] OSD_REAL w1 = weights[6];
[A] weights[9] += w1;
[A] weights[2] = weights[6] = 0.0f;
[A] OSD_REAL w0 = weights[11];
[A] weights[9] += w0;
[A] OSD_REAL w1 = weights[10];
[A] weights[7] += w1;
[A] weights[11] = weights[10] = 0.0f;
[A] Osd_boundBasisBoxSplineTri(
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wP, 12),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDs, 12),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDt, 12),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDss, 12),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDst, 12),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDtt, 12)) {
[A] Osd_adjustBoxSplineTriBoundaryWeights(boundary, wP);
[A] Osd_adjustBoxSplineTriBoundaryWeights(boundary, wDs);
[A] Osd_adjustBoxSplineTriBoundaryWeights(boundary, wDt);
[A] Osd_adjustBoxSplineTriBoundaryWeights(boundary, wDss);
[A] Osd_adjustBoxSplineTriBoundaryWeights(boundary, wDst);
[A] Osd_adjustBoxSplineTriBoundaryWeights(boundary, wDtt);
[A] // }  // namespace
[A] Osd_EvalBasisBoxSplineTri(OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wP, 12),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDs, 12),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDt, 12),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDss, 12),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDst, 12),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDtt, 12)) {
[A] OSD_REAL stMonomials[15];
[A] Osd_evalBivariateMonomialsQuartic(s, t, stMonomials);
[A] Osd_evalBoxSplineTriDerivWeights(stMonomials, 0, 0, wP);
[A] Osd_evalBoxSplineTriDerivWeights(stMonomials, 1, 0, wDs);
[A] Osd_evalBoxSplineTriDerivWeights(stMonomials, 0, 1, wDt);
[A] Osd_evalBoxSplineTriDerivWeights(stMonomials, 2, 0, wDss);
[A] Osd_evalBoxSplineTriDerivWeights(stMonomials, 1, 1, wDst);
[A] Osd_evalBoxSplineTriDerivWeights(stMonomials, 0, 2, wDtt);
[A] return 12;
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(
[A] OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t, int ds, int dt,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wB, 15)) {
[A] OSD_REAL u  = s;
[A] OSD_REAL v  = t;
[A] OSD_REAL w  = 1 - u - v;
[A] OSD_REAL uu = u * u;
[A] OSD_REAL vv = v * v;
[A] OSD_REAL ww = w * w;
[A] OSD_REAL uv = u * v;
[A] OSD_REAL vw = v * w;
[A] OSD_REAL uw = u * w;
[A] wB[0]  =      ww * ww;
[A] wB[1]  =  4 * uw * ww;
[A] wB[2]  =  6 * uw * uw;
[A] wB[3]  =  4 * uw * uu;
[A] wB[4]  =      uu * uu;
[A] wB[5]  =  4 * vw * ww;
[A] wB[6]  = 12 * ww * uv;
[A] wB[7]  = 12 * uu * vw;
[A] wB[8]  =  4 * uv * uu;
[A] wB[9]  =  6 * vw * vw;
[A] wB[10] = 12 * vv * uw;
[A] wB[11] =  6 * uv * uv;
[A] wB[12] =  4 * vw * vv;
[A] wB[13] =  4 * uv * vv;
[A] wB[14] =      vv * vv;
[A] if (ds == 1) {
[A] wB[0]  =  -4 * ww * w;
[A] wB[1]  =   4 * ww * (w - 3 * u);
[A] wB[2]  =  12 * uw * (w - u);
[A] wB[3]  =   4 * uu * (3 * w - u);
[A] wB[4]  =   4 * uu * u;
[A] wB[5]  = -12 * vw * w;
[A] wB[6]  =  12 * vw * (w - 2 * u);
[A] wB[7]  =  12 * uv * (2 * w - u);
[A] wB[8]  =  12 * uv * u;
[A] wB[9]  = -12 * vv * w;
[A] wB[10] =  12 * vv * (w - u);
[A] wB[11] =  12 * vv * u;
[A] wB[12] =  -4 * vv * v;
[A] wB[13] =   4 * vv * v;
[A] wB[14] =   0;
[A] wB[1]  = -12 * ww * u;
[A] wB[2]  = -12 * uu * w;
[A] wB[3]  =  -4 * uu * u;
[A] wB[4]  =   0;
[A] wB[5]  =   4 * ww * (w - 3 * v);
[A] wB[6]  =  12 * uw * (w - 2 * v);
[A] wB[7]  =  12 * uu * (w - v);
[A] wB[8]  =   4 * uu * u;
[A] wB[9]  =  12 * vw * (w - v);
[A] wB[10] =  12 * uv * (2 * w - v);
[A] wB[11] =  12 * uv * u;;
[A] wB[12] =   4 * vv * (3 * w - v);
[A] wB[13] =  12 * vv * u;
[A] wB[14] =   4 * vv * v;
[A] wB[0]  =  12 * ww;
[A] wB[1]  =  24 * (uw - ww);
[A] wB[2]  =  12 * (uu - 4 * uw + ww);
[A] wB[3]  =  24 * (uw - uu);
[A] wB[4]  =  12 * uu;
[A] wB[5]  =  24 * vw;
[A] wB[6]  =  24 * (uv - 2 * vw);
[A] wB[7]  =  24 * (vw - 2 * uv);
[A] wB[8]  =  24 * uv;
[A] wB[9]  =  12 * vv;
[A] wB[10] = -24 * vv;
[A] wB[11] =  12 * vv;
[A] wB[12] =   0;
[A] wB[13] =   0;
[A] wB[1]  =  24 * uw;
[A] wB[2]  =  12 * uu;
[A] wB[3]  =   0;
[A] wB[5]  =  24 * (vw - ww);
[A] wB[6]  =  24 * (uv - 2 * uw);
[A] wB[7]  = -24 * uu;
[A] wB[8]  =   0;
[A] wB[9]  =  12 * (vv - 4 * vw + ww);
[A] wB[10] =  24 * (uw - 2 * uv);
[A] wB[11] =  12 * uu;
[A] wB[12] =  24 * (vw - vv);
[A] wB[13] =  24 * uv;
[A] wB[14] =  12 * vv;
[A] wB[3]  = -12 * uu;
[A] wB[13] =  12 * vv;
[A] wB[11] =  24 * uv;
[A] wB[1]  =  24 * uw - wB[0];
[A] wB[2]  = -24 * uw - wB[3];
[A] wB[5]  =  24 * vw - wB[0];
[A] wB[6]  = -24 * vw + wB[11] - wB[1];
[A] wB[8]  = - wB[3];
[A] wB[7]  = -(wB[11] + wB[2]);
[A] wB[9]  =   wB[13] - wB[5] - wB[0];
[A] wB[10] = -(wB[9] + wB[11]);
[A] wB[12] = - wB[13];
[A] Osd_EvalBasisBezierTri(OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wP, 15),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDs, 15),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDt, 15),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDss, 15),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDst, 15),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDtt, 15)) {
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 0, 0, wP);
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 1, 0, wDs);
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 0, 1, wDt);
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 2, 0, wDss);
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 1, 1, wDst);
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 0, 2, wDtt);
[A] return 15;
[A] //  Expanding a set of 15 Bezier basis functions for the 6 (3 pairs) of
[A] //  rational weights for the 18 Gregory basis functions:
[A] Osd_convertBezierWeightsToGregory(
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wB, 15),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, rG,  6),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wG, 18)) {
[A] wG[0]  = wB[0];
[A] wG[1]  = wB[1];
[A] wG[2]  = wB[5];
[A] wG[3]  = wB[6] * rG[0];
[A] wG[4]  = wB[6] * rG[1];
[A] wG[5]  = wB[4];
[A] wG[6]  = wB[8];
[A] wG[7]  = wB[3];
[A] wG[8]  = wB[7] * rG[2];
[A] wG[9]  = wB[7] * rG[3];
[A] wG[10] = wB[14];
[A] wG[11] = wB[12];
[A] wG[12] = wB[13];
[A] wG[13] = wB[10] * rG[4];
[A] wG[14] = wB[10] * rG[5];
[A] wG[15] = wB[2];
[A] wG[16] = wB[11];
[A] wG[17] = wB[9];
[A] Osd_EvalBasisGregoryTri(OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wP, 18),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDs, 18),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDt, 18),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDss, 18),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDst, 18),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wDtt, 18)) {
[A] //  interior points will be denoted G -- so we have B(s,t) and G(s,t) (though we
[A] //  switch to barycentric (u,v,w) briefly to compute G)
[A] OSD_REAL BP[15], BDs[15], BDt[15], BDss[15], BDst[15], BDtt[15];
[A] OSD_REAL G[6] = OSD_ARRAY_6(OSD_REAL, 1.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f );
[A] OSD_REAL u = s;
[A] OSD_REAL v = t;
[A] OSD_REAL w = 1 - u - v;
[A] if ((u + v) > 0) {
[A] G[0]  = u / (u + v);
[A] G[1]  = v / (u + v);
[A] if ((v + w) > 0) {
[A] G[2] = v / (v + w);
[A] G[3] = w / (v + w);
[A] if ((w + u) > 0) {
[A] G[4] = w / (w + u);
[A] G[5] = u / (w + u);
[A] //  Compute Bezier basis functions and convert, adjusting interior points:
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 0, 0, BP);
[A] Osd_convertBezierWeightsToGregory(BP, G, wP);
[A] //  TBD -- ifdef OPENSUBDIV_GREGORY_EVAL_TRUE_DERIVATIVES
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 1, 0, BDs);
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 0, 1, BDt);
[A] Osd_convertBezierWeightsToGregory(BDs, G, wDs);
[A] Osd_convertBezierWeightsToGregory(BDt, G, wDt);
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 2, 0, BDss);
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 1, 1, BDst);
[A] Osd_evalBezierTriDerivWeights(s, t, 0, 2, BDtt);
[A] Osd_convertBezierWeightsToGregory(BDss, G, wDss);
[A] Osd_convertBezierWeightsToGregory(BDst, G, wDst);
[A] Osd_convertBezierWeightsToGregory(BDtt, G, wDtt);
[A] return 18;
[A] // The following functions are low-level internal methods which
[A] // were exposed in earlier releases, but were never intended to
[A] // be part of the supported public API.
[A] //  Deprecated -- prefer use of OsdEvaluatePatchBasis() and
[A] //  OsdEvaluatePatchBasisNormalized() methods.

[A] OsdGetBoxSplineWeights(
[A] float s, float t,
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, wP, 12)) {
[A] OsdAdjustBoundaryWeights(
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, sWeights, 4),
[A] OSD_INOUT_ARRAY(OSD_REAL, tWeights, 4)) {
[A] tWeights[2] -= tWeights[0];
[A] tWeights[1] += tWeights[0] * 2.0f;
[A] tWeights[0]  = 0.0f;
[A] sWeights[1] -= sWeights[3];
[A] sWeights[2] += sWeights[3] * 2.0f;
[A] sWeights[3]  = 0.0f;
[A] tWeights[1] -= tWeights[3];
[A] tWeights[2] += tWeights[3] * 2.0f;
[A] tWeights[3]  = 0.0f;
[A] sWeights[2] -= sWeights[0];
[A] sWeights[1] += sWeights[0] * 2.0f;
[A] sWeights[0]  = 0.0f;
[A] OsdComputeTensorProductPatchWeights(
[A] float dScale, int boundary,
[A] OSD_IN_ARRAY(float, sWeights, 4),
[A] OSD_IN_ARRAY(float, tWeights, 4),
[A] OSD_IN_ARRAY(float, dsWeights, 4),
[A] OSD_IN_ARRAY(float, dtWeights, 4),
[A] OSD_IN_ARRAY(float, dssWeights, 4),
[A] OSD_IN_ARRAY(float, dttWeights, 4),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(float, wP, 16),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(float, wDs, 16),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(float, wDt, 16),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(float, wDss, 16),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(float, wDst, 16),
[A] OSD_OUT_ARRAY(float, wDtt, 16)) {
[A] // Compute the tensor product weight of the (s,t) basis function
[A] // corresponding to each control vertex:
[A] OsdAdjustBoundaryWeights(boundary, sWeights, tWeights);
[A] // Compute the tensor product weight of the differentiated (s,t) basis
[A] // function corresponding to each control vertex (scaled accordingly):
[A] OsdAdjustBoundaryWeights(boundary, dsWeights, dtWeights);
[A] wDs[4*i+j] = dsWeights[j] * tWeights[i] * dScale;
[A] wDt[4*i+j] = sWeights[j] * dtWeights[i] * dScale;
[A] // Compute the tensor product weight of appropriate differentiated
[A] // (s,t) basis functions for each control vertex (scaled accordingly):
[A] float d2Scale = dScale * dScale;
[A] OsdAdjustBoundaryWeights(boundary, dssWeights, dttWeights);
[A] wDss[4*i+j] = dssWeights[j] * tWeights[i] * d2Scale;
[A] wDst[4*i+j] = dsWeights[j] * dtWeights[i] * d2Scale;
[A] wDtt[4*i+j] = sWeights[j] * dttWeights[i] * d2Scale;
[A] OsdGetBilinearPatchWeights(
[A] OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t, OSD_REAL d1Scale,
[A] int nPoints = Osd_EvalBasisLinear(s, t, wP, wDs, wDt, wDss, wDst, wDtt);
[A] for (int i = 0; i < nPoints; ++i) {
[A] wDs[i] *= d1Scale;
[A] wDt[i] *= d1Scale;
[A] OSD_REAL d2Scale = d1Scale * d1Scale;
[A] wDss[i] *= d2Scale;
[A] wDst[i] *= d2Scale;
[A] wDtt[i] *= d2Scale;
[A] OsdGetBSplinePatchWeights(
[A] OSD_REAL s, OSD_REAL t, OSD_REAL d1Scale, int boundary,
[A] int nPoints = Osd_EvalBasisBSpline(s, t, wP, wDs, wDt, wDss, wDst, wDtt);
[A] Osd_boundBasisBSpline(boundary, wP, wDs, wDt, wDss, wDst, wDtt);
[A] OsdGetBezierPatchWeights(
[A] int nPoints = Osd_EvalBasisBezier(s, t, wP, wDs, wDt, wDss, wDst, wDtt);
[A] OsdGetGregoryPatchWeights(

[A] #endif /* OPENSUBDIV3_OSD_PATCH_BASIS_H */
